Account & Payment: Account Basics

Change Your Deezer Username, Email Or Password

There may come a time when you want to change your username, password or email. At Deezer, we take your security very seriously, so we’ve added an extra security step to confirm that it’s really you making the request to change your email or password. Jump to section: - How to change your email or password - How to change your username

Check out the steps below to see how to change your details: Tip: You can also change your password here by clicking this link #### How to change your email or password 1. Go to your settings by selecting your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application 2. Select Account 3. Select Change your email address or Change password 4. Select SEND CODE The six digit security code will be sent to the email address that is on your screen 5. Check your email for the six digit code 6. Back on the Deezer app, enter the six digit code 7. Select CONTINUE or RESEND CODE to start the process again from step five 8. Enter your new email address or password 9. Re-enter your new email address or password 10. Select SAVE CHANGES

Note: If you don’t have access to this email, you’ll need to have it changed by one of our agents. For security reasons, the code to reset your password will expire after 10 minutes. Once expired, you’ll need to request a new one. 1. Go to your settings by selecting your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application 2. Select Account management 3. Select Change your email address or Change password 4. Select SEND CODE The six digit security code will be sent to the email address that is on your screen 5. Check your email for the six digit code 6. Back on the Deezer app, enter the six digit code 7. Select CONTINUE or RESEND CODE to start the process again from step five 8. Enter your new email address or password 9. Re-enter your new email address or password 10. Select SAVE CHANGES

Note: If you don’t have access to this email, you’ll need to have it changed by one of our agents. For security reasons, the code to reset your password will expire after 10 minutes. Once expired, you’ll need to request a new one. 1. Go to your settings by selecting your profile icon at the top right hand side of your application 2. Select Account settings 3. Under the My Information section, find your email address or password and select the pencil icon to edit 4. Select SEND CODE The six digit security code will be sent to the email address that is on your screen 5. Check your email for the six digit code 6. Back on deezer.com, enter the six digit code 7. Select CONTINUE or RESEND CODE to start the process again from step five 8. Enter your new email address or password 9. Re-enter your new email address or password 10. Select SAVE CHANGES

Note: If you don’t have access to this email, you’ll need to have it changed by one of our agents. For security reasons, the code to reset your password will expire after 10 minutes. Once expired, you’ll need to request a new one. #### How to change your username Unfortunately, you cannot change your username on iOS devices. You will need to log in to deezer.com to change your username. 1. Go to your settings by selecting your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application 2. Select Account management 3. Select your profile at the top of the page 4. Enter a new username 5. Select OK

  1. Go to your settings by selecting your profile icon at the top right hand side of your application
  2. Select Account Settings
  3. Under Deezer information that can be seen by other Deezer users, in the Username field, enter a new username

Create A Deezer Account

We are so glad you’ve decided to join Deezer, our music streaming service where you can listen to millions of songs and podcasts. Creating a Deezer account is really simple and can be created online at deezer.com or via sign up once you have downloaded the mobile app. Once you have created an account, you will immediately be signed up to the Deezer Free plan by default - but whilst you are here, take a look at our Premium, Family or Duo subscription which offers even more at a brilliant price. Jump to section: - How to sign up to Deezer - Tips for a secure password - FAQ

Note: To learn how to download the mobile app, visit Download The Deezer App #### How to sign up to Deezer 1. Go todeezer.com/register or open the app 2. Enter your email address. Double check you’ve entered it correctly! 3. Enter a username and secure password 4. Enter your identity and age 5. Select Sign up

Note: You must be at least 16 years old to create an account. Account Admins with Deezer Familyplans can create child profiles for underage users #### Tips for a secure password - Your password must have at least 8 characters, 1 uppercase, 1 lowercase, 1 special character (for example, !”£$%£^&) and 1 number - Never use the same password for more than one account within a plan - Don’t share your Deezer password

Note: You can change your login details and username at any time #### FAQ Please enter a new email or use the password reset to recover your account with Forgot your password? Tip: We have a handy article that will teach you more about creating an account with Facebook or Google here.

Find Your Deezer User ID

Jump to section: - How to find your User ID

Every Deezer profile has a User ID attached to it. It’s a 10 digit number assigned to your account. If you ever need to get in touch with our Support team or if you forget your login details, we can easily find your account with your User ID. We suggest that you make a note of it just in case. #### How to find your User ID 1. Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application 2. Click on the Settings (image: Settings.png) cog icon at the top right hand side of your application 3. Within the Preferences section, select Account 4. Under Your account details you will see your User ID and registered email address

  1. Go to Settings (image: Settings.png)
  2. Select Account management (image: AccountManagement.svg) At the top of the page, you’ll see the following information about your account:
  3. Your Deezer username
  4. Your registered email address
  5. Your User ID

Identify Fake Emails And Phishing

Our commitment to you and the security of your personal data is of utmost importance to us. This article will explain some common security terms, help you to secure your personal information and protect yourself against phishing scams. Jump to section: - What is Phishing? - How to identify a fake or phishing email - What to do if you receive a phishing email - Still not sure if a Deezer email is fake? - What to do if you have shared your personal information with a scammer

What is Phishing?

Phishing is usually done through email, ads, or by sites that look similar to sites that you already use. For example, someone who is phishing might send you an email that appears to be sent from Deezer so that you’ll share information about your bank account. Phishing emails will often ask for personal information or security details such as: - Usernames and passwords, including password changes - Social Security numbers - Bank account numbers - PINs (Personal Identification Numbers) - Credit card numbers - Your mother’s maiden name - Your birthday

Warning: Deezer will never ask you to provide this type of information in an email, so don’t give this information out #### How to identify a fake or phishing email Phishing emails are designed to look as authentic as possible, so at first glance they can look like a legitimate email. When trying to identify a phishing email, the details are usually what give it away. Keep an eye out for these red flags that can help you to spot a fraudulent email. Here are some of the most common things to look for: - The email has ended up in your spam folder. Many email services can detect the more obvious phishing attempts and will filter them to your spam folder to warn you - The email asks you to log in to verify your details - The email address and the sender name do not match (@deezer.com) - The email uses an old brand logo - Check the email header to see who the email is from. Most email services will allow you to see more detailed information about who sent the email by clicking on the email address. You should: - Check that the domain (e.g. @deezer.com) matches the sender name - Check that the email is signed by the correct domain

What to do if you receive a phishing email

  • Do not click any links until you’ve checked them first

Note: On a PC or Mac, you can hover over the link to inspect it. You’ll usually see the destination displayed at the bottom of your browser window. You can also use a link scanner. There are several sites out there that will scan a link and give you a risk assessment, such as URLVOID or Norton Safe web - Do not download anything from an email or linked by an email - Do not reply to the email or share your personal information in any manner - Report the email as spam in your email software

Still not sure if a Deezer email is fake?

If you’re still not sure of an email’s legitimacy, treat it like it’s fraudulent and simply delete it. Don’t respond to it, click any links, or download anything in the email. #### What to do if you have shared your personal information with a scammer If you have shared your password: - Immediately reset the password of your Deezer account in your settings - Change the passwords for your other subscriptions and accounts if they are the same or similar to the one you use for Deezer

If you have shared your credit card details: - Contact your bank immediately to terminate your credit card - File a complaint with the police

Log In And Out Of Deezer

Logging in and out of your Deezer account is really simple, and this article will guide you through the process of doing so. If you still have questions, please find our links at the end of the article for more help. Jump to section: - How to log in - How to log out - How to log in to Deezer from an email - Still need help?

(image: LogInAndOutOfDeezerHeader_EN.jpg) #### How to log in Logging in to Deezer is nice and simple. Just follow these steps: Tip: If you haven’t got an account, you can learn how to create one here. 1. Open the Deezer app 2. Enter the email address associated with your account or log in with Apple ID, Facebook or Google 3. If required, enter your password

Tip: Learn more about signing in with your Apple ID and Facebook or Google 1. Open the Deezer app 2. Enter the email address associated with your account or log in with Facebook or Google 3. If required, enter your password

Tip: Learn more about signing in with your Facebook or Google 1. Go to deezer.com 2. Select Log in  3. Enter the email address and password associated with your account or log in with Apple ID, Facebook or Google

Tip: Learn more about signing in with your Apple ID and Facebook or Google #### How to log out 1. Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application 2. Go to the Settings (image: Settings.png) cog at the top right hand side of your application 3. Scroll to the bottom of the page and then select LOG OUT (image: Logout_EN.png)

  2. Go to the Settings (image: Settings.png) cog at the top right hand side of your application

  3. Scroll to the bottom of the page and then select LOG OUT (image: Logout_EN.png)

  4. Go to your profile (image: Profile.png)

  5. Select Log out

How to log in to Deezer from an email

We know that having to enter your password can be annoying, so we’ve created a way for you to log in from your email inbox. Note: This is not available on desktop 1. On the Deezer Welcome screen, enter your email address 2. On the Your password screen, select LOG IN WITHOUT PASSWORD 3. Select OPEN EMAIL. Alternatively, you can manually navigate to your emails 4. Within the email we sent, select Log in now

See our troubleshooting guide or, better yet, ask one of our superusers in the Deezer Community. Deezer values your feedback. Join the conversation at Deezer Community. Account & Payment: Deezer Family

Add Someone As A Member To A Deezer Family Account

With a Deezer Family plan, 1 admin and 5 other members can enjoy unlimited music on demand so long as you live in the same household. There are several different ways to add a new user to a Family account - this article will explain the difference between them and how to add your Family members, including creating a Kid profile. Jump to section: - How to add an independent account to a Family account - How to add a profile to a Family account - How to create a Deezer Kid profile - How to remove an account

With a Deezer Family plan, there must be one admin user, and up to 5 other members can enjoy the benefit of a Family subscription. Those 5 can be added as independent accounts with their own email and password or as Profiles, which share the same login details as the admin. You can create Kid profiles for children 12 and under, and you can also hide explicit content on profiles, which we recommend doing for children between the ages of 12 and 16. Personal music libraries and recommendations are kept separate, ensuring members only listen to their favorite content. Note: You must be logged in to the admin account to add someone to your Family #### How to add an independent account to a Family account Note: If the member you’re inviting already has a Deezer account, they can accept the invite and log in with their existing credentials. To join the Family plan, their account must be on the free version (no active subscription). If they currently have an active subscription, they’ll need to wait for it to end before accepting the invite. Note: If the member you are inviting is already associated with a Deezer Family account with another user, they will not be able to join until they remove their association with another account 1. In the top left hand side of your application, you will see your initial next to Home - when you select this you will see your account settings 2. Select Plan management and underneath the title Multi-account management, select Manage my accounts 3. Select Add a member 4. Select Send an invite 5. Select how you would like to send the invite link

Note: Invite links will expire after 1 day. If the invite link has expired, the Admin will need to send a new invite

Note: If the member you’re inviting already has a Deezer account, they can accept the invite and log in with their existing credentials. To join the Family plan, their account must be on the free version (no active subscription). If they currently have an active subscription, they’ll need to wait for it to end before accepting the invite. Select Send an invite 5. Select how you would like to send the invite link

How to add a Profile to a Family account

Profiles share the same login details as the admin account. To make a Profile with the same log-in details as the Admin: 1. In the top left hand side of your application, you will see your initial next to Home - when you select this you will see your account settings 2. Select Plan management and underneath the title Multi-account management, select Manage my accounts 3. Select Add a member 4. Select Create a profile 5. Enter the profile details for the member 6. Select Create profile

To make a Profile with the same log-in details as the Admin: 1. In the top left hand side of your application, you will see your initial next to Home - when you select this you will see your account settings 2. Select Members 3. Select Add a member 4. Select Create a profile 5. Enter the profile details for the member 6. Select Create profile

Note: You are able to create a new Profile from the first page you see when you log in 1. Go to your profile (image: Profile.png) in the top right hand side of the application 2. Under the icon with the + sign, Select Members

Note: You can also get to the Members page when you log in to your account 1. Select Create a profile 2. Enter the profile details for the member 3. Select Create

How to create a Deezer Kid profile

You can create a Kid profile for children under 12 that contains only child-friendly content. The process to create a Kid profile is the same as a standard Profile with one additional step: - Select Kid profile (image: Slider.png)

Note: You will need to select the checkbox on the desktop version. You will also need to review our Privacy Policy Note: Learn more about connecting devices and explicit content #### How to remove an account As your kids grow, or your family changes, you may wish to remove someone from the Family account so they can have their own subscription. You can only do this if you are the owner of the Family plan. To remove a family member 1. In the top left hand side of your application, you will see your initial next to Home - when you select this you will see your account settings 2. Select Plan management and underneath the title Multi-account management, select Manage my accounts 3. Select Remove a member

Note: For Profiles you will see Remove this profile opposed to ‘Members’ - selecting remove will permanently delete that profile. To remove a family member 1. In the top left hand side of your application, you will see your initial next to Home - when you select this you will see your account settings 2. Select Members 3. Select the verticle ellipses and from the opened up settings, select Remove a member

Note: For Profiles you will see Remove this profile opposed to ‘Members’ - selecting remove will permanently delete that profile. 1. Go to your profile by tapping on your profile icon on the top right hand side of the app 2. Select Members 3. Click Manage Members 4. Select the member you wish to remove 5. Select Remove

Note: For Profiles you will see Remove this profile opposed to ‘Members’ - selecting remove will permanently delete that profile. Once the family member is removed from the Family plan, they can sign up for whichever subscription meets their requirements in the normal sign up process. Deezer values your feedback. Delete Or Remove A Deezer Family Member

There may become a time where you need to remove, from Deezer Family, an account or profile. Maybe your children are all grown up and wants their own Deezer subscription? We know it is the end of an era, but it’s really simple to do - this article will guide you through the process of deleting an account or profile. Jump to section: - How to delete a profile from your Family - How to remove an independent account from your Family - FAQ

To delete or remove a member from your Family account you must be logged in to the admin account. #### How to delete a profile from your Family We recommend that any content that you want to save is transferred to another account before you delete a Profile. 1. Go to your settings by selecting your profile icon in the top left hand side of the application 2. Select Plan management 3. Under the section Multi-account management, select Manage my accounts 4. On the account you wish to delete, select the three dots next to the user 5. Select Delete this profile 6. A pop up will appear asking you to confirm whether you wish to delete the user

  1. Go to your settings by selecting your profile icon in the top left hand side of the application

  2. Select Members

  3. Select the three dots (image: ThreeDotsHorizontal.svg) against the user profile you wish to delete icon

  4. Select Delete this profile

  5. A pop up will appear asking you to confirm whether you wish to delete the user

  6. Go to your settings by selecting your profile icon in the top right hand side of the application

  7. Select Members

  8. Select Manage members

  9. Select the edit (image: Edit.svg) icon next to the member you want to delete

  10. Select Delete this profile

  11. Enter the username of the Profile

  12. Select Delete to confirm

How to remove an independent account from your Family

Note: An independent account can only be removed from your Family. It cannot be deleted 1. Go to your settings by selecting your profile icon in the top left hand side of the application 2. Select Plan management 3. Under the section Multi-account management, select Manage my accounts 4. On the account you wish to delete, select the three dots next to the user 5. Select Remove this member 6. Select OK to confirm

Note: Removed independent accounts will revert back to Deezer Free 1. Go to your settings by selecting your profile icon in the top left hand side of the application 2. Select Members 3. Select the three dots (image: ThreeDotsHorizontal.svg) against the user profile you wish to delete icon 4. Select Remove this member 5. Select OK to confirm

Note: Removed independent accounts will revert back to Deezer Free 1. Go to your settings by selecting your profile icon in the top right hand side of the application 2. Select Members 3. Select Manage members 4. Select the edit (image: Edit.svg) icon next to the member you want to delete 5. Select Remove 6. Enter the username of the independent account 7. Select Remove to confirm

Note: Removed independent accounts will revert back to Deezer Free. The account owner can subscribe to Deezer Premium or Deezer Family at any time to access music on demand and offline listening #### FAQ When an Admin deletes a Profile, the member’s favorites, recommendations, playlists and personal listening history will be permanently deleted. This is why we recommend converting profiles into independent accounts or transferring playlists before the Admin deletes a Profile or kid-only profile to ensure the content is not lost. When an Independent account is removed, the account will revert back to Deezer Free. Independent account owners can subscribe to any Deezer plan to access playlists, music on demand and offline listening.

Hide Explicit Content For Deezer Family Members

Making sure the content your family listens to is appropriate for their age is really important, which is why Deezer’s Hide explicit content tool can help you manage Deezer Family members, giving you more control of the music and podcasts your family can access on Deezer. When this setting is activated, any content featuring the Explicit tag will be hidden for your Family member. Jump to section: - Availability - How to hide explicit content - Helpful links - FAQ

Note: Hide explicit content can be switched on or off by the Admin for Family member profiles and Independent accounts. By default, this feature is switched off and can only be accessed and managed by the Admin #### Availability This feature is available for Deezer Family subscribers worldwide on web, mobile and desktop devices. Note: Explicit content is automatically hidden for Kid profiles #### How to hide explicit content 1. Log in to the Admin account 2. Go to your profile, which is in the top left hand side of the application 3. Click on the Settings cog at the top right hand side of the application 4. Select Plan Management from the list of Settings 5. Underneath the section Multi-account management select Manage my accounts. A list of accounts associated with the Family account will appear 6. Select the three dots … next to the profile you want to hide explicit content for and select Edit profile 7. Next to Hide explicit content click on the toggle to enable 8. Select Save to confirm your settings

  1. Log in to the Admin account

  2. Go to your profile, which is in the top left hand side of the application

  3. Click on the Settings cog at the top right hand side of the application

  4. Select Members

  5. Select the three dots (image: ThreeDotsVertical.svg) next to the profile you want to hide explicit content for and select Edit profile

  6. Next to Hide explicit content click on the toggle to enable

  7. Select Save to confirm your settings

  8. Log in to the Admin account

  9. Go to your profile in the top right hand side of the application  (image: Profile.png)

  10. Select Members

  11. Select Manage members

  12. Select the edit icon of the Family member you would like to manage

  13. Select Hide explicit content (image: Checkbox.svg)

  14. Select Save

Helpful links

Learn more about Explicit content settings and how to join as an Independent account. #### FAQ You cannot hide Explicit content for Kids profiles as they are automatically inaccessible for Kids profiles aged 12 and under. Join And Use Deezer Family With An Independent Account

An independent account allows you to log in with your own credentials with complete privacy, meaning you can keep all your favorite content and playlists even if your Family admin unsubscribes from the Deezer Family plan. In this article, we outline how to join Deezer Family with an independent account and how to convert your Family Profile into an independent account. Jump to section: - How to join a Family as an independent account - How to convert an existing Family profile into an independent account - If you want to change to a paid subscription of your own - FAQ

How to join a Family as an independent account

Ensure that the Admin account and the account that you want to join with are in the same country. Note: You must have a Deezer Free account in order to join a Family. This means that if you are a Deezer Premium subscriber, you must unsubscribe from it and wait for your current period to end before joining 1. An invite will have been sent to you from the Admin of the Family account. Select the link 2. Select ACCEPT INVITE 3. Enter your login details or select Sign up if you don’t have a Deezer account A message will appear asking you to verify whether the correct account is selected 4. Select YES IT’S MY ACCOUNT if it is your account Note: Select CHANGE ACCOUNT if an incorrect account is displayed

How to convert an existing Family profile into an independent account

  1. When you are logged in with your profile, go to the top left hand side of your application and select your profile icon to open your settings
  2. Select Account
  3. Under the heading Login & security you will see a button that says Add my credentials
  4. Enter an email address and password for your account

Alternatively, if you are the admin and want to convert another profile: 1. When you are logged in with the admin profile, go to your profile by selecting your icon at the top left hand side of the application 2. Select Plan management 3. Underneath the section titled Multi-account management, select Manage my accounts 4. Click on the three dots to the right of the profile you wish to create an independent account for 5. In the settings pop up, select Convert this profile into an independent account 6. Enter an email address and password for the account Note: When the account admin has converted a profile into an independent account, the owner of that independent account will be logged out. They will need to use the credentials entered by the admin to log back in

Warning: You are able to convert profiles into independent accounts even if the Family account no longer has an active subscription. If this happens, the owner of the independent account will no longer be part of the Family account. They will have their own credentials as a Deezer Free account 1. When you are logged in with your profile, go to the left hand side of your application and select your profile icon to open your settings 2. Select Account management 3. Under the heading Login & security you will see a button that says Add my credentials 4. Enter an email address and password for your account

Alternatively, if you are the admin and want to convert another profile: 1. When you are logged in with the admin profile, go to your profile by going to the left hand side of your application and selecting your profile icon 2. Select Members 3. Select the three dots (image: ThreeDotsHorizontal.svg) menu next to the profile you want to convert 4. Select Convert this profile into an independent account 5. Enter an email address and password for the account Note: When the account admin has converted a profile into an independent account, the owner of that independent account will be logged out. They will need to use the credentials entered by the admin to log back in

Warning: You are able to convert profiles into independent accounts even if the Family account no longer has an active subscription. If this happens, the owner of the independent account will no longer be part of the Family account. They will have their own credentials as a Deezer Free account 1. Once logged into your profile, go to the top right hand side of your application and select your profile icon 2. Select Account settings 3. Within the My Information tab, select Set as independent account 4. Choose whether you would like to connect with Facebook or connect with Google 5. Enter your Facebook or Google password Note: If you’re already logged in, it may not be necessary to enter your password. If the email address attached to the Facebook or Google account is already in use, an error message will be displayed, and you’ll need to delete the existing Deezer account associated with this email or enter a different email Once linked, you’ll automatically be logged out from the Admin account and logged into your independent account

Tip: Remember to log out and log back in again on all devices with your Facebook or Google credentials #### If you want to change to a paid subscription of your own If at some point the Family account holder cancels their subscription or moves over to an individual one, don’t worry! You can still use the profile you have set up independently to your own subscription, without losing all your favorite content. Once the Family account holder has cancelled their subscription, you will be able to subscribe to your choice of Deezer service by logging into your account and selecting Account Management under your profile settings. #### FAQ Invites expire after 30 days. Ask the Admin to resend the invite. A maximum of 5 members can be added to a Deezer Family plan on top of the Admin (subscriber). Please ask the Admin to delete or remove members and resend you the invite. If this is not possible, you may want to consider registering for one of our other Deezer plans. The plan you are trying to join is not based in the same country as you, so you cannot complete the journey. You’ll need to cancel your existing Premium plan first before you can join. Don’t worry, you’ll keep all of your favorite and playlists.

Log In To Deezer Family

Logging into your account on Deezer Family differs depending on how you have set up your account as a member - either you will have an independent account, or a profile. This article will explain how to log in, regardless of which type of account you have. Jump to section: - How to log in with an independent account - How to log in with a Profile - How to switch Profiles - FAQ

How to log in with an independent account

Independent accounts have their own login details. If you were added to the Family with an independent account, you will need to enter the login details for your account to log in. Other independent accounts and profiles will not be able to access your account. Note: The Family admin may have changed your profile into an independent account for you. You should ask the admin for the credentials to log in. You can then change your password within the app. Tip: You can log in without your password by entering your email address on the login page and then selecting LOG IN WITHOUT PASSWORD. You can then change your password within the app. #### How to log in with a Profile If you are set up with a profile, you will need to enter the same login details used by the admin of the Family account. Once you have logged in, you will be able to pick your profile from the members page. Note: You are also able to go to Settings and select Members to pick another profile #### How to switch Profiles 1. Log in to your account 2. Select the profile you would like to access

Note: If you are already logged in on mobile, you can view other profiles by going to Settings and selecting Members. If you are already logged in on Desktop, you can view other profiles by selecting your profile photo and then selecting Members #### FAQ If you have a profile, see our Amazon Alexa, Google Nest Audio and Sonos articles on how to switch member profiles. Note: An independent account can be accessed and linked independently with its own login credentials Note: This only applies to profiles (not independent accounts)

Can’t remember making an Deezer account? Or sure you have one but have no idea which email address to log in with? If you are taken to the Create a password page, you do not have a Deezer account associated with that email address If you are taken to the Your password page, you do have a Deezer account associated with that email address

If you are taken to the Create a password page, you do not have a Deezer account associated with that email address If you are taken to the Your password page, you do have a Deezer account associated with that email address

Delete Your Deezer Account

There may come a time when you need to delete your Deezer account. We completely understand and we want to ensure you know what deleting your account involves. All data, settings, favorite tracks and playlists will be permanently deleted. Any remaining subscription time will also be permanently deleted. Jump to section: - Have you considered canceling instead? - How to delete your account - How to delete an account created with Facebook or Google

Have you considered canceling instead?

If you have a Deezer Premium, Deezer Duo, Deezer Student, Deezer Family, Deezer HiFi or Deezer Family HiFi account, you can cancel your subscription and your account will automatically return to Deezer Free. Just keep in mind you won’t be able to listen offline, skip tracks or enjoy advertisement-free music. Tip: Learn more about canceling here #### How to delete your account Note: An account with an active subscription cannot be deleted. If you have an active subscription, you must first cancel it and wait until the account reverts to the free plan. Only then will you be able to delete the account 1. Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application 2. Select the Settings cog at the top right side of your application 3. Under Preferences, select the Account section 4. Scroll to the bottom of the screen and click on Delete my account 5. You will need to confirm your identity before cancelation is complete - to receive your code click Send code. This will be sent to the email address registered with your Deezer account 6. Enter the 6 digit code sent to your email address and select Continue. You also have the option to Resend code - we recommend you check all your email folders before requesting a new code 7. A screen will appear advising you that you are about to permanently delete your account and will lose access, data and subscriptions on Deezer. You will need to select the tick box to confirm you understand, and to delete your account, select Delete everything

  1. Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application

  2. Select the Settings cog icon at the top right hand side of your application

  3. Select Manage plan

  4. Select Delete my Account

  5. You will need to confirm your identity before cancelation is complete - to receive your code click Send code. This will be sent to the email address registered with your Deezer account

  6. Enter the 6 digit code sent to your email address and select Continue. You also have the option to Resend code - we recommend you check all your email folders before requesting a new code

  7. A screen will appear advising you that you are about to permanently delete your account and will lose access, data and subscriptions on Deezer. You will need to select the tick box to confirm you understand, and to delete your account, select Delete everything

  8. Go to your Profile

  9. Select Account Settings

  10. Scroll down to the bottom of the screen and select Delete my account

  11. Enter your Deezer password and select Delete my account. You will receive a validation email

  12. Click on the link to delete your account

Warning: Deleting your account is irreversible. You will not be able to recover it #### How to delete an account created with Facebook or Google If you created your account via Facebook or Google, you will need to use the Deezer password that was sent to you when you created your account. If you cannot remember your password, you can reset it and follow the above instructions.

Select Manage my subscription 4. Select Payment history to see your invoices

  3. Select Manage my subscription

  4. Select Payment history to see your invoices

Language Settings on Deezer

The language that you use Deezer in is very important, just like the language settings on all your other apps. You may have accidentally set the wrong language, or maybe you’re learning a new language and want to see how well you know it! Having the ability to change your language settings is vitally important, and this article will explain how. Jump to section: - How to change your language

How to change your language

You can change Deezer’s language directly from your device settings. Steps to change the Deezer app language: - Open your Settings app on your iPhone - Scroll down to Apps - Select Deezer - Select Language (sometimes shown as Preferred Language) - Select the language you want Deezer to use - Close and reopen the Deezer app for the change to apply

Note: If you don’t see the Language option, Deezer is currently set to follow your device’s main language You can change Deezer’s language directly from your device settings. Note: Some steps only work on Android 14 or later. Learn how to check your Android version here Note: Apps set to Follow system default will use the first supported language from your device’s language list Steps to change the Deezer app language: - On your Android device, select your Settings app - Select System → Languages → App Languages - From the list, select Deezer - Choose your preferred language

Note: You can only change the language for apps that support this feature 1. Go to your profile (image: Profile.png) 2. Select Account Settings 3. Within the My Information tab, under Private information, select your language from the Language dropdown 4. Select Save

Note: Changing your language settings will only work on a web browser, and not on the desktop version of the app

Would you like to request a tax invoice?

Please note that in order to issue your tax invoice, you must consent to sharing your personal information with us, as this data is required to process your request.

Transfer Music To Deezer From Another Service

We understand you don’t want to spend hours rebuilding your lovingly curated playlists and music library when you’ve just moved over to Deezer. Were you a previous user of Spotify? Obsessed with listening to songs on YouTube? No problem! With Tune My Musicyou can directly transfer your existing music library from all major platforms to Deezer in just a few simple steps. After all, the bigger your music library, the better our recommendations will be for you! (image: tunemymusiclogo.PNG) Jump to section: - How to transfer my music to Deezer

How to transfer my music to Deezer

  1. Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application
  2. Select the Settings cog icon at the top right hand side of your application
  3. Under the Preferences section, select Music
  4. Within Content preferences, select Import my music We will redirect you to Tune My Music
  5. Select the music platform you want to transfer from
  6. Follow the instructions on your screen to transfer your music
  7. Select Go back to Deezer once the transfer is complete We will send you back to the Deezer app

Tip: Look for troubleshooting tips on Tune My Music’s FAQ Warning: Tune My Music is partnered with us. We do not operate Tune My Music 1. Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application 2. Select the Settings cog icon at the top right hand side of your application 3. Under Content preferences, select Transfer your library We will redirect you to Tune My Music 4. Select the music platform you want to transfer from 5. Follow the instructions on your screen to transfer your music 6. Select Go back to Deezer once the transfer is complete We will send you back to the Deezer app

Tip: Look for troubleshooting tips on Tune My Music’s FAQ Warning: Tune My Music is partnered with us. We do not operate Tune My Music 1. Go to your profile (image: Profile.png) 2. Select Account settings 3. Select the Transfer your library tab Note: This may be located under the More (image: DropdownArrow.svg) dropdown due to your screen size 4. Select SELECT A SERVICE 5. Follow the instructions on your screen 6. Select the music you want to transfer 7. Select Transfer to Deezer

Tip: Look for troubleshooting tips on Tune My Music’s FAQ Warning: Tune My Music is partnered with us. We do not operate Tune My Music #### FAQ Spotify, Apple Music, YouTube, YouTube Music, Tidal, Amazon Music, SoundCloud, iTunes, Qobuz, Moodagent, Napster, Beatport, Beatsource, KKBOX, Last.fm, SoundMachine. Warning: If you do not make the transfer using the Deezer app, you will only be able to transfer a limited number of items before having to pay. Yes, absolutely! Tune My Music uses public APIs to retrieve music from one service to send to another. Legally there is no problem with this. Music streaming services would of course have the right to close their public API, but it’s unlikely. No, Tune My Music only imports musical content. Deezer Gift Cards

Stuck for a gift for a family member or friend that seems to have everything but loves streaming music, discovering new tracks, podcasts and more? Have you ever thought about gifting them a subscription to Deezer? Give the gift of unlimited music on demand to family and friends. Better yet, you can connect with them as a friend and use all the fun features through your Profile! Is there a better present?! Jump to section: - In-store Deezer gift card locations - Online Deezer digital gift code locations - How to purchase a Deezer digital gift card online - How to redeem your gift card - Deezer gift card not working?

Deezer gift cards come in two forms: digital and physical. Each gift card has a code that gives access to Deezer subscriptions for a minimum of 1 month or a maximum of 12 months (depending on your country). Note: Deezer gift cards are only available for Deezer Premiumand are valid for 12 months from the date of purchase. Gifts codes can be combined with each other and with Deezer subscriptions, except if you have subscribed with Apple or if you have a deal with your mobile operator. The music catalogue available depends on the country of activation Note: You will not be able to use a Deezer gift card if: - Your subscription was started through a Partner offer - You pay for Deezer through Google Play or Apple (in-app purchase)

To redeem a gift card, you’ll need a regular Deezer subscription paid directly on our website with an accepted payment method Warning: If you’re already subscribed, using a gift code will push back your next payment date #### In-store Deezer gift card locations You can buy physical Deezer gift cards in these stores in select countries: - Country: Popular stores - France: Darty, Fnac

Online Deezer digital gift code locations

You can buy a digital gift card in these countries: Afghanistan, Algeria, Austria, Australia, Bahrain, Belgium, Bangladesh, Benin, Bolivia, Brazil, Cameroon, Canada, Chile, Colombia, Costa Rica, The Democratic Republic of the Congo, Czech Republic, Denmark, Dominican Republic, Ecuador, Egypt, Estonia, Finland, France, Gabon, Germany, Ghana, Greece, Guatemala, Honduras, Hungary, Iceland, Indonesia, Ireland, Israel, Italy, Jamaica, Jordan, Kazakhstan, Kuwait, Latvia, Lebanon, Lithuania, Luxembourg, Madagascar, Malaysia, Morocco, Mauritius, Mexico, Netherlands, Nicaragua, Nigeria, Norway, New Zealand, Oman, Panama, Paraguay, Peru, Philippines, Poland, Puerto Rico, Portugal, Qatar, Romania, Russia, Salvador, Saudi Arabia, Singapore, Slovakia, South Africa, South Korea, Spain, Sweden, Switzerland, Tanzania, Thailand, Tunisia, United Arab Emirates, UK, USA, Uruguay, Uzbekistan, Venezuela, Vietnam and Zimbabwe #### How to purchase a Deezer digital gift card online 1. Open the Deezer gift card link here 2. Select the length of duration 3. Enter your payment details 4. Select Confirm You will receive two emails, one summarizing your order, and the other containing the code

You can combine and activate as many gift cards as you wish on top of your existing subscription, but this excludes if you have subscribed via Apple or Google, if you have a Deezer Family or Deezer Duo subscription still active or if you are signed up to Deezer via partnership such as Orange. In any of these cases, you will need to cancel your current Deezer subscription and wait until your account reverts to Deezer Free before you can use the Deezer Premium gift code. Note: Deezer Gift Cards are only valid for a Premium subscription, and this excludes Deezer Family and Deezer Duo Note: You must register or log in to a Deezer account to buy a digital gift card. #### How to redeem your gift card 1. Go to deezer.com/gift 2. Enter the code you received in your email or on the back of the physical card

Note: Make sure you’re logged in to the correct account when redeeming your gift card. If you pay for your Deezer subscription plan through Google Play or iTunes, you’ll need to cancel via those stores and then redeem your Deezer gift card #### Deezer gift card not working? If you’re having trouble redeeming you gift card, please check: - Your gift card is redeemable in your country. Any gift cards you purchase – physical or digital – will only be redeemable in the country of your payment method - your gift card hasn’t been redeemed already - you haven’t applied your gift card to a different Deezer account - you have entered your voucher code correctly

Tip: Watch out for these characters: I / i / 1 (one); L / l (lower case L); O / o / 0 (zero); S / s / 5 (five)

How to rectify a refused payment with Deezer

We all know sometimes payments don’t go through and the hassle involved in trying to get it sorted again. But don’t worry - we have created a payment checklist of the most common reasons a payment fails, and what you can do to rectify it! If you can’t solve your issue using our article, see the bottom section for more advice on further help from our Deezer agents. Jump to section: - Payment checklist - How to update your payment method

Payment checklist

Before contacting us about a failed payment, we recommend checking that: - Your card has not expired, been made invalid or locked by your bank - You have sufficient funds and are not overdrawn - Your payment method matches the country of your subscription - You’ve entered the 3-digit CVV/CVC security code correctly - Your card can make overseas, online and recurring transactions - You meet the requirements for a trial or promotion and have not previously subscribed Warning: You may not be eligible to an offer if you have previously subscribed to a trial or promotion. However, you can still subscribe to the standard Deezer plans

Note: If you’re subscribed though your mobile provider or Apple’s App Store, you’ll need to contact your third-party provider #### How to update your payment method You can see how to update or change your payment method here. Note: In some rare cases, it could be an issue on our side affecting certain customers. Please check out News & Announcements for our latest updates. If a payment is refused, you will received an email asking you to update the method of payment. Deezer values your feedback. Join the conversation at Deezer Community. Want more insight to the topics explored in this article? Find out more at Payment issues on Deezer

Pay For Deezer Through Apple FAQs

These are the most frequently asked questions for those Deezer users that have subscribed through the Apple App Store. #### FAQ Whenever a subscription is made through the App Store, the price you pay is higher because it is made up of our subscription price, local taxes and Apple’s commission. As a result, the subscription price for users via the App Store is slightly higher than subscribing directly to Deezer. Anyone who subscribed or re-subscribed to Deezer through the App Store or Apple’s in-app payment (IAP) system. All of our customers, including iOS users, can still pay the standard prices for Deezer by subscribing through Deezer.com. If you do subscribe directly, it is easier to make changes to your subscription plan.

Try Deezer For Free FAQ

In this FAQ article, we cover some of the most commonly asked questions about trying out Deezer Premium for free. Tip: Check out deezer.com/offers to see if you’re eligible to try Deezer Premium for free Note: If you’ve previously subscribed to one of our mobile trials or other promotions, you will not be eligible to try Deezer Premium for free #### FAQ Yes, depending on your region and eligibility, you can try Deezer for free for 30 or 90 days. You’ll need to enter your payment details before you can try it. At the end of the free period, you’ll automatically be charged for the monthly renewal of your subscription unless you choose to cancel and revert back to Deezer Free. If you choose to cancel, any content you’ve downloaded will not be available to listen to offline and you won’t have access to Deezer paid features. Note: In select countries, we offer Deezer Student and Deezer Family trials for 3 months If you’re having problems trying Deezer for free with your iOS device, please see our troubleshooting article on Apple payments.

Unexpected Charges on Deezer FAQs

In this FAQ article, we explain why you might see unexpected charges on your account. Occasionally, you may see a small charge to verify your details are accurate. This charge appears as pending and rarely leaves your account. Once the authorization process is completed, the payment is immediately released. Deezer subscriptions are charged on a monthly basis. If you find a second payment has been taken in the same month, there are a couple of reasons why this might happen: - You have accidently created a second Deezer account with another email address - You have subscribed twice using two different Deezer accounts with two different payment methods, such as credit cards, iTunes, or your mobile provider

Firstly, check if you have two accounts. Secondly, check if you have more than one payment method. Finally, cancel the subscription you do not need as soon as possible Adyen is one of our payment processor partners. If you see Adyen on your bank statement and would like to confirm it’s a legitimate payment to Deezer, you can so through their website here. Deezer values your feedback. Join the conversation at Deezer Community. Want more insight to the topics explored in this article? Find out more at Payment issues on Deezer

Why Isn’t My Deezer Subscription Plan Working?

Are you hearing adverts when you are signed up to Premium? Are you unable to shuffle songs but pay for a Family subscription? Occasionally issues like this occur, but we want to help you reinstate your Deezer subscription with some troubleshooting tips. If you’re a Deezer Premium, Deezer Duo, Deezer Family or Deezer HiFi user and you discover that you cannot use all your paid features, here are some of the most common reasons why your Deezer subscription might not be working. - You’ve logged into another account. If you have more than one Deezer account, please check that you’ve logged into the correct account - Your trial has ended. Check your subscription status using the steps in the section below to see if your free trial offer has ended - You have signed up via Apple but have not activated your offer - in this instance, you will need to contact us with a copy of your invoice detailing your payment history to reinstate - Your payment was refused. If you’re paying for Deezer with a debit or credit card, your payment should be completed almost immediately.  

Tip: If all of the above are correct and your subscription still has not been activated, your account may need to be reviewed by our Support team. You can also check our article on refused payment for troubleshooting tips

Note: Cash payments or bank transfers can take up to 72 hours. Brazilian customer payments can take up to three working days to be cleared. Please contact us here with your PIN number

Account & Payment: Sharing & Security

Cookies on Deezer websites

Most, if not all websites will store ‘cookies’ of your personal information which shapes your internet browsing experience, and Deezer does the same. However, we understand and acknowledge the importance of consent when and how these cookies are used, and want to ensure that you are happy with what information you share with us. Jump to section: - What are cookies? - What cookies am I consenting to?

What are cookies?

Cookies are small text files that websites send to your browser and are stored on your computer. These cookies are used to track your online activity and formulate personalization of your browsing experience. They can also be used to shape user preferences and tailored content, such as ecommerce sites recalling your previously purchased items and suggesting similar, as well as remembering login credentials to avoid having to sign in each time you visit a website. However, cookies can raise privacy concerns as they track and utilise your personal data and online behaviour. That said, cookies are important for security purposes, such as authentication cookies, which provides layers of security on your online accounts. #### What cookies am I consenting to? You can select to Allow all cookies if you are happy with Deezer storing your personal data to personalize content and ads, to provide social media features and analyze traffic. However, if you want to curate which cookies are stored, you can maximise the window to select the cookies you are happy accepting. These will range from cookies that store marketing/advertising, statistical and security preferences and more information about each individual cookie can be viewed. If at any time, you are unsure of what kind of data we capture and want to find out more information, you can view our Privacy Policy here.

How to Request your data

Your data is important to us, so we’ve made it easy for you to access the information we hold. If you’re looking to access your personal data, you’re in the right place. Follow these simple steps to retrieve your information. Step 1: Request Your Data - Log in to your account on our website. - In your private Information there is a button titled Personal Data “click” on this

Step 2: Confirm Your Request - A pop-up window will appear, outlining what your export will include.

  • If everything looks good, click “request my data” to proceed.

Step 3: Verify Your Identity - Check your email inbox for a message from us. - Inside, you’ll find a secure session code. - Enter this code on our website to continue.

Step 4: Get Confirmation - Once you’ve entered the code, you’ll receive a confirmation email from us.

Step 5: Access Your Data - After 7 days * you’ll receive another email. - This email will contain a link to download your data. - Simply log in to your account to access and download your information.

The data will remain available for download for 30 days, after which you can make a new data request.

Third Party Data Breach

Related article: Handling Data Breach Inquiries - 2019 (2022) Third-Party Incident In November 2022, we were made aware that a data leak occurred in the systems of a former service provider that Deezer used until 2020. As a result, a snapshot of our users’ non-sensitive information was exposed. The exposed data includes basic information such as first and last names, date of birth, and your email address. To be clear, no information regarding passwords or payment details has been discovered. The provider in question breached several of its contractual obligations, notably by retaining the data after the termination of the contract with Deezer (which is why the data set is dated 2019), despite the precautions taken by the latter to ensure that the data was destroyed. The failure of this provider to delete Deezer’s data (despite providing confirmation of destruction in 2020) led to the hacking in 2022. Deezer’s systems and databases have not been affected and remain secure. Deezer has not violated any data protection regulations and is also a victim of its former service provider’s gross negligence, as well as the maliciousness of hackers. Deezer is committed to protecting the privacy of its users and providing the highest level of security to protect the personal data we collect and process to provide our services. Deezer has always taken state-of-the-art security measures to protect its users’ personal data. Deezer has always endeavored to use the most advanced security measures, just as it requires its suppliers to comply with the strictest security standards. We have even more strengthened existing security measures and continue to closely monitoring the situation to detect potential fraud and inform our users if necessary. All our customer service agents are aware of the incident, so they can assist you in case of difficulty. However, we encourage you to change your password and to be vigilant, as the compromised data can be used for phishing purposes. A phishing attack involves impersonating a business or organization to convince a potential victim to click on a link in an email or text message. Most often, the victim is asked to update their credit card details to continue using an online subscription. How to recognize a phishing attempt - Check the sender’s email address. Cybercriminals grossly impersonate companies and organizations. For example: noreply@deeezer.com - Look out for typos. Phishing messages often contain spelling or syntax errors - Be wary if you’re asked to provide your data. Neither Deezer, nor any other serious business or organization, will contact you by email to ask you for your password or credit card details

What to do if you receive a phishing email - Don’t click on any of the links included in the email - Don’t reply to the email or share your personal information in any manner - Don’t open any file attachments included in the email - Report the email as spam in your email software

What to do if you have shared your personal information with a scammer If you have shared your password: - Immediately reset the password of your Deezer account in your settings (Go to Settings, select Account management, select Change password) - Change the passwords for your other subscriptions and accounts if they are the same or like the one you use for Deezer

If you have shared your credit card details: - Contact your bank immediately to terminate your credit card - File a complaint with the police

You can access your Apple private relay email in your iOS device settings following these steps: 1. Go to Apple ID 2. Select Password & Security 3. Select Apple ID Logins 4. Select Deezer

Alternatively, you can find this email address on deezer.com with these steps: 1. Go to your profile (image: Profile.png) 2. Select Account Settings 3. Within the My information tab your email address will appear under Log in

You’ll still be able to log in to different devices with the email and password you used to create your Deezer account. 1. From the Deezer Login page, select Apple (image: AppleLogo.svg) You will be asked if you want to sign in with your Apple ID 2. Select Continue

Note: If no match is found, you will be asked to create a new account. If you do not wish to create a new account, please make sure to change the email on your Deezer account to match your Apple ID #### How to sign in to Deezer on other devices when Apple ID is enabled If your account was created using your Apple ID after June 2020, then you will need to create a Deezer password in order to log in on other devices, such as Amazon Alexa. You can reset your password here. If you’ve chosen to Hide my email, you will also need to make sure email forwarding is enabled in order to receive the password reset email. Note: You’ll need to enter the email you used to register your account. If you opted to hide your email during the registration process, you’ll need to enter the relay email Tip: If you created your Deezer account before June 2020, you can still use your previous Deezer login credentials to sign in on other devices, such as Amazon Alexa or Google Home #### How to link your Apple ID to Deezer from the Deezer app You can also link or unlink your Apple ID by going to Share settings from inside the Deezer app with these steps: 1. Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application 2. Select the Settings cog icon (image: Settings.png) at the top right hand side of your application 3. Meanwhile, for further information about 360 Reality Audio, visit here 360 by Deezer is our app dedicated to Deezer users that allows you to hear songs recorded in 360 Reality Audio. 360 Reality Audio maximizes the spatial sound field by creating multiple virtual speakers and converting instruments into independent objects that can be placed in different positions to create a truly immersive experience for listeners. You’ll also be able to cast content from the Deezer 360 app to certain Sony speakers in 360 Reality Audio quality or cast to any device in regular quality. #### Requirements - iOS version 11 or above - Android version Lollipop or above - Deezer Premium - A 360 Reality Audio compatible device

Cancel Your Deezer Subscription

You can cancel your subscription at any time through the Deezer app or website. If you cancel your Deezer Premium subscription, you’ll still have access to your Premium features until the end of your current subscription period. After that, your account will revert to Deezer Free and any downloaded music will no longer be playable offline. All of your favorite tracks, playlists and content will still be visible, but some features, like unlimited skips, downloading and offline listening, are only available to paid subscribers. All downloaded content will become grey and unplayable. Jump to section: - Have you thought about switching to a different plan? - How to cancel your subscription - Still need help?

If you are subscribed to a Deezer Premium plan and you want to connect to a Deezer Family plan, you’ll need to cancel first. Once your subscription has reverted back to Deezer Free, you can connect your Deezer account as an independent account using the invite link. Note: If you have a Deezer Free account the process is the same, but there is no need to cancel first Yes, we offer annual payment options, but this is not available in all countries. Please check the link to see the offers available in your country. Yes, existing Deezer Family Profiles can become independent accounts with their own login credentials. You can find out how to convert profiles into independent accounts here. With Deezer Family, you can link or pair up to 13 devices across your accounts - the Admin of the account will be able to link three devices, and the remaining accounts/profiles will be able to link two. These paired devices are the devices in which you can download music to, so may be your phone or desktop. However, you can stream from as many devices as you want. If you have, for instance, Google Home, this will not count as a paired device but a streaming device, and there is no limit to the number of devices you stream from. Ensure all listeners access the correct Profile to prevent listening error messages from occurring while music is being played on another device or page. You may also need to reset your password. If you cancel your Deezer Family plan, your account will revert to Deezer Free and all Profiles will be suspended. Independent accounts will revert back to Deezer Free, keeping all their favorites, playlists and recommendations (except downloaded content). Once they’ve reverted back, they have the possibility to switch to any Deezer plan to access playlists, music on demand and offline listening. Deezer values your feedback. Deezer Free

Explore a rich catalog with Deezer Free, offering millions of songs! The platform is 100% free, powered by ads to support the artists and content you love. You’ll find everything from global hits to local music tracks, podcasts and radio. Deezer Free is designed to give you control over your listening, enhancing the free experience. You’ll benefit from a customized experience from the moment you download the app, sign up, and you can pick and play any song on select personalized playlists, your Daily playlist and a selection of Editorial playlists. Jump to section: - What’s included? - How to sign up to Deezer Free - Compatible devices - How to switch to Deezer Free - Deezer plan comparisons - FAQ

(image: Desktop (1).png) Note: This page describes the Deezer Free mobile app experience. Deezer Free web users can select the individual songs they want to listen to, with skip limits in place for certain content #### What’s included? Here are some of the perks of a Deezer Free subscription: - Personalized recommendations in your Made for you section - Songcatcher, the listening tool that helps you capture and save your favorite tunes - Unlimited skips on a number of Daily playlists - Unlimited skips on a personalized selection of Editorial playlists - Shuffle the top songs of an artist when you select and play them Note: Selecting individual tracks from an artist page will play a 30-second preview - Flow , the infinite self-learning feature that plays and recommends songs based on your listening preferences - Flow Moods , an extension of Flow that allows you to pick a particular mood which then creates an infinite playlist based on that mood Note: Choose between Love, Workout, Chill, Sad, Focus, and Party - Flow Genres, another extension of Flow that allows you to pick a particular genre you want to listen to Note: There are an incredible 32 genres to select from!

Note: The Lyrics feature is only available for users on paid subscriptions Our music acquisition is negotiated between artists & right-holders. How to sign up to Deezer Free

Signing up to Deezer Free is easy. Simply create an account online or download the app to start listening to music. When you sign in for the first time, you will be asked to choose at least 3 artists for more personalized recommendations. You can select from the artists or genres displayed. Alternatively, you can search for your favorite artists. Deezer Free is available in: United States, Brazil, Germany, France, Canada, Great Britain, UAE, Belgium, Ireland, Saudi Arabia, Turkey, and Ukraine. Please note that in the United States, Deezer Free is available on mobile devices only. Desktop users in the US can only play 30-second song previews. #### How to switch to Deezer Free You can switch from any plan (Deezer Premium, Deezer Duo, or Deezer Family) to Deezer Free. All you have to do is cancel your subscription. Your account will revert to the Deezer Free service on your next billing date. If you are currently subscribed to a Deezer Premium plan through another company (e.g. iTunes) or through one of our partners (e.g. a mobile provider), you must cancel your subscription through your provider first, then switch. Note: Any content you’ve previously downloaded for offline listening will not be available #### Deezer plan comparisons Within the Deezer mobile app, on the Premium tab, you’ll find a list of Deezer plans and a comparison tool that allows you to compare Deezer Free with other plans. Tip: View our comparison article here With our other plans, you can use your Deezer account on all your devices: mobile, sound systems, TVs and game consoles. You can also enjoy offline listening and ad-free music. Tip: Visit the Deezer offers pages for more information #### FAQ There are several ways to improve your Deezer recommendations. - Listen to Flow, recommendations and playlists - Make sure you favorite tracks, albums and artists using the heart (image: favourites.png) icon so that Deezer learns your listening habits - Exclude a song or artist using the unhappy emoji (image: Unhappy.svg) - Favorite more artists to give your homepage a more personalized feel

On the mobile app, playlists displaying a play (image: Play.svg) icon on the cover can be listened to with unlimited skips, so you can pick the exact song you want to play. These playlists are updated regularly, at least once a week. With the rest of the catalogue, you can Shuffle (image: Shuffle.png) the music. This allows you up to six skips per hour. Lyrics is a feature available for Deezer paid subscribers, so those who have subscriptions to Premium, Duo, Family and Student. These pop ups are part of the Deezer Free service, offering the opportunity to sign up to one of our subscription services.

Fraudulent websites offering Deezer plans

We’ve noticed some websites out there claiming to offer Deezer Premium subscriptions for free or promoting unauthorized subscriptions. While a good deal is tempting, these sites are not connected to us at all - and some people have unfortunately been scammed from these websites ending up paying for services that do not exist. Want to stay safe? Here’s what to watch out for: - Websites offering “free Premium converters” - Suspiciously cheap subscription prices - Deals that seem too good to be true - Websites that offer ability to download content, which is an illegal act as it violates our terms and conditions

We are constantly monitoring the web for new websites that try to fraud our users, many of which have been taken down. Unfortunately, these websites often resurface with different or slightly altered addresses. If you’re not sure about an offer you’ve seen, the best thing to do is: - Check our officialsubscription prices directly on Deezer - Reach out to our team - we’re here to help!

Remember: The only way to get genuine Deezer subscriptions is through us or our official partners

High Fidelity (HiFi) on Deezer

HiFi is the best way to listen to music! In most countries, HiFi is automatically included in Deezer Premium, Deezer Duo and Deezer Family subscriptions. However, there are exceptions where HiFi is still offered as a standalone subscription, allowing you to enjoy music in the clearest quality yet. For countries where HiFi is not automatically included, subscribers will still have access to High Quality (320 kb/s) sound for all their songs at no extra cost. Jump to section: - Requirements - Compatible devices - How to set up your audio quality - HiFi Classics - FAQ

Requirements

HiFi requires a minimum speed of 5 Mb/s to enjoy FLAC streaming. The higher your speed, the better your FLAC streams will be. Note: Audio is encoded with 16-Bit/44.1 kHz FLAC to deliver the very best listening quality Our music acquisition is negotiated between artists & right-holders. Don’t forget that if you have an active subscription you can upload the song to your own mp3 section on Deezer and then you can add it to playlists. The step by step guide is here. #### Compatible devices Deezer HiFi users can stream FLAC quality on Desktop and across select TV, iOS and Android devices. Tip: Select devices may have a built-in Deezer app. Look for the Deezer HiFi (image: HiFiBadge.png) icon on our devices page to see what models are supported. #### How to set up your audio quality You can set up your High Fidelity settings by following these steps: 1. Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application 2. Select the Settings cog in the top right hand side of your application 3. From the Preferences section, select Music 4. Under Audio preferences, select Choose audio quality 5. The application will open a new screen allowing you to select your audio quality preferences

You can set up your High Fidelity settings by following these steps: 1. Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application 2. Select the Settings cog icon from the top right hand side of your application 3. Select Audio 4. Under Streaming quality and Download, configure your audio settings

Make sure your streaming quality is correctly configured on your device. On IOS devices, go to your profile → Settings → Music → Choose audio quality, then select the option for Mobile, Wi-Fi, or Google Cast. On Android, go to your profile → Settings → Streaming quality, then choose the desired option for Mobile, Wi-Fi, or Google Cast. On Desktop, click the audio icon in the player and select the preferred quality. Note: Each streaming option can be set to a different audio quality While it’s recommended that you use one of the devices with the HiFi  (image: HiFiBadge.png) icon listed on the devices page, you can use another device as long as you have the necessary software.
