How to subscribe to Deezer

Welcome to the Deezer family! You can look forward to listening to millions of songs, quality podcasts and radio stations to discover. Not only that, but with a subscription to Deezer you can listen to all your favorite tunes offline and create playlists. You can even identify tracks or sing along to your favorite songs with built-in features like SongCatcher and Lyrics Jump to section: - Subscription types - How to subscribe - FAQ

Subscription types

We offer various subscriptions depending on your needs: - Deezer Premium - an individual user account - Deezer Duo- a subscription that allows two members of the same household to use Deezer Premium under one ownership - Deezer Family - a subscription for the whole Family! Create six family accounts, including kids and listen on up to 13 devices! - Deezer Student - a subscription for users that meet Student eligibility

Whatever your needs, you will be able to find a Deezer subscription that suits you (and your family!). #### How to subscribe Subscribing to one of our Deezer plans is really simple and you can do it one of two ways: - Visit www.deezer.com/offers** and select the subscription that best fits your needs - Download** and install our Deezer application on your device to view our offers

In both circumstances you will need to create a Deezer account - to find out more on how to do this, visit our support site article Create a Deezer Account for more information. You can also sign up to Deezer using either your Facebook or Google account. When you sign up, you will be asked to provide a valid email address and select a password. You can also fill out Profile information. Once you have selected the subscription plan you wish to sign up for, you will be asked to provide details of your preferred payment method - either a credit/debit card or Paypal. Find out more about supported payment methods here. When your payment method has been selected and confirmed, your subscription will start immediately! Note: Payments can differ depending on which country you are based in - please find more information here If you are already a subscriber on Deezer Free or Premium and you would like to move to a plan that offers you more music for the rest of your family, you can join Deezer Family by following these steps: - Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application - Select the Settings cog at the top right hand side of your application - Select Account management - Click on Manage my subscription - depending on your current plan, select Subscribe or Switch.

Note: If you subscribe on either a Family or Duo subscription and want to downgrade, you will first need to cancel the current subscription and once your account reverts to Free, subscribe again to the plan of your choice Tip: New users may be eligible for a Try & Buy offer that includes a free trial period (usually between one to three months) - this trial period will still require a valid payment method associated with the account but will not be charged until the trial period has expired. You cannot activate a new offer if you have been a Deezer subscriber in the last 12 months Note: Subscriptions will automatically renew each calendar month unless cancelled 48 hours before renewal is due - if you subscribe via Google Play or Apple you will need to manage your subscription through the respective platform #### FAQ If you are looking, for example, to upgrade your Deezer Premium subscription to a Deezer Family, or want to downgrade Family to Premium, please check out our article here

Upgrading or Downgrading your Deezer Subscription

Are your kids desperate to listen to their own music? Maybe you’ve got a new flatmate and want to share a Deezer subscription! Or perhaps you’ve just started university and want to benefit from a Student subscription. Deezer makes it simple to upgrade or downgrade your subscription at any time, giving you access to different account types. Jump to section: - What type of subscriptions are available? - Upgrading from Free - Upgrading from Premium to Family/Duo - Downgrading from Family/Duo to Premium/Free

What type of subscriptions are available? 

We offer various subscriptions depending on your needs: - Deezer Premium - an individual user account - Deezer Duo - a subscription that allows two members of the same household to use Deezer Premium under one ownership - Deezer Family - a subscription for the whole Family! Create six family accounts, including kids and listen on up to 13 devices! - Deezer Student - a subscription for users that meet Student eligibility

Whatever your needs, you will be able to find a Deezer subscription that suits you (and your family!). #### Upgrading from Free Decided you and your partner want to join Duo? Or started university and want a Student account? No problem, upgrading your account is simple. Your first step should be visiting www.deezer.com/offers and select the plan that best fits your needs. Tip: New users may be eligible for a Try & Buy offer that includes a free trial period (usually between one to three months) - this trial period will still require a valid payment method associated with the account but will not be charged until the trial period has expired Note: Only new users will be eligible for a Try & Buy offer. If you are a returning user, you will be charged at the normal subscription rate Once you’ve selected your offer, you’ll have to sign in with your login credentials (email username and password) and enter your payment details to continue. Note: Payments can differ depending on which country you are based in - please find more information here After this, enjoy the music! #### Upgrading from Premium to Family/Duo If you are already a subscriber on Deezer Free or Premium and you would like to move to a plan that offers you music for another member of your household, or the rest of your family, you can join Deezer Duo or Deezer Family by following these steps: - Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application - Select the Settings cog at the top right hand side of your application - Select Account management - Click on Manage my subscription - depending on your current plan, select Subscribe or Switch

Note: If you are changing your subscription and are subscribing through Apple, your new subscription will only take effect after the current subscription expires, and although you will be charged immediately for the subscription, you will not have access to the benefits straight away. We recommend in this instance that you cancel your current subscription first, wait for it to expire and then proceed with an upgrade. #### Downgrading from Family/Duo to Premium/Free Have your kids all flown the nest? Or maybe your housemate has moved out? It’s no problem if you need to downgrade your subscription. If you subscribe on either a Family or Duo subscription and want to downgrade, you will first need to cancel the current subscription and once your account reverts to Free, subscribe again to the plan of your choice. Note: If you subscribe to Deezer through your Apple account, you can only cancel directly with Apple. Find out how to cancel your subscription here To cancel your subscription: 1. Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application 2. Select the Settings cog icon at the top right hand side of your application 3. Under the Subscription section, select Plan management 4. Select Subscription details & invoices 5. Select Cancel my subscription 6. Select the reason for canceling and then select Confirm

  1. Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application

  2. Select the Settings cog icon at the top right hand side of your application

  3. Under My Subscription Plan, select Manage my subscription

  4. Scroll down until you get to Want to cancel your subscription? and select

  5. Select Cancel subscription

  6. Go to your profile (image: Profile.png)

  7. Select Account Settings

  8. Select Manage my subscription

  9. Select Cancel my subscription

  10. Select the reason for canceling and then select Confirm

Note: Subscriptions will automatically renew each calendar month unless cancelled 48 hours before renewal is due - if you subscribe via Google Play or Apple you will need to manage your subscription through the respective platform

Bodega Aurrera and Walmart Partnership with Deezer in Mexico

This article will provide you with up-to-date and comprehensive information on the newest Deezer’s partnership with Walmart Mexico y Centroamérica, including an overview of Walmart and Deezer Premium, the offers available, how to activate and manage your account or if you need to cancel the Deezer subscription. You will also find a list of frequently asked questions and troubleshooting tips should you have a problem with your account. #### Who is Walmart de México y Centroamérica? It is one of the largest retail chains in Latin headquartered in Mexico City, The business considers different store formats including Bodega Aurrera, Bodega Aurrera Express, Mi Bodega Aurrera, Sam’s Club, Walmart Express and Walmart Supercenter. Walmart Mexico is also known for its significant market influence, the company offers a range of goods and services from groceries and household products to electronics and apparel, it has an e-commerce presence, allowing customers to shop online. #### Bodega Aurrera Beneficios and Walmart Beneficios | Reward program Beneficios is a program in an affiliate account that grants benefits by accumulating purchases amount as a progress indicator. ##### How does the program work? To earn benefits from your purchases, you must provide your cell phone number at checkout or register your purchases online. The benefits will be delivered at checkout when giving the number in a purchase, or could be activated trough the landing page bodegaaurrera.com.mx/beneficios and walmart.com.mx/beneficios based on the advance given by registered purchases. If you make a return, the refunded amount will be deducted from your accumulated balance. #### What is Deezer Premium? With Deezer Premium, you can listen to all your music offline and create playlists, without the hassle of adverts. You can even identify tracks, sing along to your favourite songs and play music quizzes with other users with built-in features like SongCatcher, Lyrics and Music Quizzes. All these features are available to you, and you can experience it all with Hi-Fi sound! #### What kind of Deezer offers are available at Walmart and where? If you are a Bodega Aurrera Beneficios or Walmart Beneficios customer, you can get the benefit of Deezer Premium with a 15% or 25% discount over the actual price, depending on the amount you spend in the store. This discount comes after 1 month free of Deezer Premium. Currently, these offers are only available in Mexico for all Bodega Aurrera Beneficios or Walmart Beneficios registered customers. #### How to access the Deezer Premium benefit and activate it To be eligible to get this benefit, Bodega Aurrera Beneficios or Walmart Beneficios customers will need a redeemable benefit to select and activate the Deezer offer: - Option 1 - 1 month free Deezer Premium and in the next months 15% discount over full price - Option 2 - 1 month free Deezer Premium and in the next months 25% discount over full price

Note: You can accumulate purchases in Walmart Beneficios when you buy in-store or online in the following brands - Bodega Aurrera, Bodega Aurrera Express, Mi Bodega Aurrera, Walmart Express and Walmart Supercenter. Purchases in Sam’s Club are not considered for the program. The process to start accumulating your purchase is simple: 1. When you pay for your purchase, provide your phone number 2. Complete your registration online or through SMS 3. Accumulate more purchases online or in physical stores to be eligible for more and better benefits 4. Activate the Deezer Premium benefit and start enjoying it 5. You will receive an email with an activation link 6. Redeem the code by pressing the option Active my benefit 7. You will be redirected to Deezer landing page to activate the service 8. If you have a Deezer account, LOGIN with your email address and password or alternatively, if you don’t have an account with us, SIGN UP adding all the requested details 9. You will be redirected to the payment page to select your method of payment after the 1-month free you are entitled to 10. All set, you are ready to enjoy all the features of Deezer Premium!

Eligible Customers to 1-month Deezer Premium offer with 15% or 25% discount

To be able to redeem this benefit, you must fill these conditions: - Customers must participate in Walmart Beneficios or Bodega Aurrera Beneficios - Successfully managed to activate the promotional offer Deezer Premium with a 15% or 25% discount depending on the selection available for each user has achieved - Eligible customers are only entitled to 1-month free Deezer Premium - To enjoy 1-month free Deezer Premium, customers must not have previously participated in a free trial to any Deezer direct subscription service - Customers can’t have an active paid subscription or had one paid subscription in the past to enjoy 1-month free Deezer Premium - To access the Deezer Premium Service, end-users who redeem the Promotional Offer shall create a Deezer account and conclude an online contract with Deezer by accepting Deezer’s standard terms of use (or log in to their existing account)

If you cancel your Deezer Premium subscription, you’ll still have access to your Premium features until the end of your current subscription period. After that, your account will revert to Deezer Free and any downloaded music will no longer be playable offline. All of your favourite tracks, playlists and content will still be visible, but some features, like unlimited skips, downloading and offline listening, are only available to paid subscribers. All downloaded content will become grey and unplayable. Note: Deezer Free is not available in all countries, so you might not be able to listen any content, when you cancel your paid subscription. Bouygues Partnership with Deezer in France

This article will provide you with up-to-date and comprehensive information on Deezer’s partnership with Bouygues in France, including an overview of Bouygues and Deezer, how to activate your account and a list of frequently asked questions and troubleshooting tips should you encounter a problem. Jump to section: - Who are Bouygues Telecom? - Who are Deezer? - What are the available channels to subscribe to a Deezer plan via Bouygues Telecom? - How to Subscribe and use Deezer via Bouygues - Upgrade or downgrade your Deezer Plan - How to cancel your subscription - FAQS

Who are Bouygues Telecom?

Bouygues Telecom is one of France’s leading telecommunications companies, founded in 1994 as part of the larger Bouygues Group. It provides mobile phone, internet, and television services to millions of customers nationwide. Known for innovation and high-quality network coverage—including 4G, 5G, and fibre-optic internet - Bouygues Telecom focuses on customer satisfaction and sustainable digital solutions. At the end of 2024, Deezer renewed its long-term partnership with Bouygues Telecom, cementing its strategic focus on the French market and enabling continued delivery of innovative music experiences. This renewal aligns with Deezer’s new strategic plan launched in 2025, aimed at empowering users through enhanced personalization and responsible innovation in music streaming, including transparency initiatives like AI-generated music tagging. #### Who are Deezer? Deezer is a global music experience platform delivering millions of tracks, personalized playlists, exclusive shows, and Hi-fi sound quality. Deezer’s unique features include Flow for personalized discovery, synchronized lyrics, Blind Tests, and SongCatcher for music recognition. You can listen ad-free, download music for offline mode, and enjoy premium sound on all your devices. Follow the latest news on https://newsroom-deezer.com/news/ 🤩! ##### Deezer Offers via Bouygues Telecom or B&You 1 GO Bouygues Telecom customers can subscribe to Deezer Premium, Duo, or Family plans directly from their client space or while subscribing to eligible Bouygues mobile or Bbox internet plans. - Deezer Premium: €11.99/month after up to 2 months free trial, offering ad-free music and offline mode - Deezer Duo: €15.99/month after one month free trial, for two independent accounts with all Premium features - Deezer Family:€19.99/month after one month free trial, for up to six individual accounts, including a Kid’s mode option

All plans are without commitment, allowing cancellation anytime. Note: All subscription prices mentioned are based on information available as of October 2025 and may be subject to change. Please check the latest offers and pricing directly on the Bouygues Telecom or Deezer official websites for the most up-to-date details. The Deezer-Bouygues Telecom partnership offers an intuitive experience. Activation and subscription management are handled seamlessly through the Bouygues portal, ensuring quick access to personalized music experiences. #### What are the available channels to subscribe to a Deezer plan via Bouygues Telecom? Customers can purchase or activate a Deezer plan through Bouygues Telecom using several official channels. Each option allows fluid integration of Deezer billing within your Bouygues account and ensures immediate subscription activation. Bouygues Telecom Client Space (Espace Client): 1. Log into your Espace Client on https://www.bouyguestelecom.fr 2. Go to Mes options → Ajouter une option 3. Select the Deezer option (Premium, Duo, or Family) 4. Confirm your plan selection

This is the most direct and frequently used method for activation. Bouygues Telecom Mobile App 1. Open the Bouygues Telecom app (available on iOS and Android) 2. Log in with your Bouygues account credentials 3. Access the “Offers and Options” section and add the Deezer music option

The app allows you to manage all services from your smartphone, including Deezer activation. Bouygues Telecom Stores 1. Visit any Bouygues Telecom retail store across France 2. Request to add the Deezer plan that suits you better to your current Bouygues or Bbox plan

The store advisor will guide you step by step, and an activation email will follow once complete. Over the Phone 1. Call the Bouygues customer service line at 1064 (from France) or +33 660 614 614 (from abroad) 2. Request to add the Deezer plan that suits you better to your current Bouygues or Bbox plan

The support agent will guide you step by step, and an activation email will follow once complete. This ecosystem of channels gives Bouygues customers flexibility to subscribe online, via app, over the phone, or in-store—ensuring Deezer is integrated quickly into their Bouygues experience. #### How to Subscribe and use Deezer via Bouygues Log in to your Bouygues Telecom client space - Bouygues Telecom Website. DAZN Partnership with Deezer in France

Jump to section: - What is DAZN? - What is Deezer Premium? - How to activate your offer - How to cancel your subscription - Re-subscription terms

This article will provide you up to date and comprehensive information on Deezer’s partnership with DAZN, including an overview of DAZN and Deezer Premium and how to activate your account. For frequently asked questions, please click here. #### What is DAZN? DAZN is a streaming service that provides live sports events to customers. DAZN have partnered with Deezer to offer Deezer users the chance to combine music and live sports together for an exclusive monthly fee. #### What is Deezer Premium? With Deezer Premium you can listen to all your music offline and create playlists, without the hassle of adverts. You can even identify tracks, sing along to your favorite songs and play music quizzes with other users with built-in features like SongCatcher, Lyrics and Music Quizzes. All of these features are available to you, where you can experience it all with HiFi sound! #### How to activate your offer Note: This offer is only eligible for users in France, and current paid subscribers, including those on Premium, Duo and Family - so long as they are the main account holder. ##### On Deezer iOS/Android App Within the Deezer application, an offer to sign up to DAZN streaming services will appear. When selected on iOS or Android, you will be taken to a promotional page where you can select Subscribe now to continue. Doing this will subscribe to the service using your Apple Pay/Google Play billing details. Once you have completed this step, you will receive two emails - one to confirm your chosen payment method and one to confirm your subscription to DAZN with a link to a landing page where you will need to activate your offer - select this, enter your email address and create an account on DAZN to continue. Once you have done this, you will be able to start streaming sports! ##### On the web To access this offer on the Deezer webpage, select your Deezer profile icon and click Partners’ offers followed by Subscribe to DAZN where you will be directed toward the promotional page to subscribe. To continue the process, enter your payment details and select Activate. You will be sent a link via email which directs you to the DAZN account creation page where you will need to enter your personal information, after which you will be able to start streaming! Note: If your profile or account sits within an existing Family or Duo account, you will not be able to benefit from this offer - only admin accounts can sign up. Note: Deezer subscriptions and DAZN subscription dates are not correlated, therefore both payment methods can be different (e.g. you can pay for Deezer via Google Play billing, and DAZN via credit card on the web) and payment cycles (when the payment is taken from your account) can be different. You will see two separate transactions for the services, one from Deezer and one from DAZN. #### How to cancel your subscription To reiterate, the Deezer subscription and the DAZN subscription are not correlated - they are two separate subscriptions which are combined together. Therefore, if you wish to cancel one of them, there are certain conditions to be aware of. If you are planning on cancelling your Deezer subscription via your payment method of Android or credit card via webpage, your DAZN subscription will automatically be cancelled as well. Warning: If you have signed up via iOS on Apple billing, your DAZN subscription will still be active and therefore chargeable monthly, and you will need to cancel the DAZN subscription separately. Cancellation is possible from any platform (Android, iOS and webpage) If you are planning on cancelling your DAZN subscription (but not Deezer), you can do so without cancelling your Deezer subscription - this will still remain active and if you wish to cancel Deezer, you will need to go through the normal channels outlined in [link] article. Cancellation is possible from any platform (Android, iOS and webpage). ##### To cancel your DAZN subscription: Select Manage my subscription which you can find within Settings in the Deezer app - you will see your active DAZN subscription listed. To cancel, click on the active subscription and select Cancel. The subscription will end at the end of the current billing cycle. #### Re-subscription terms If you have taken up the Deezer and DAZN subscription offer before and have cancelled at any point, and you wish to resubscribe you will not be eligible for the sign up offer, but will be eligible for the monthly repeat customer subscription. It is possible to resubscribe at any time, using the same method of payment - iOS Apple billing, Google Play billing or credit card billing. The sign-up and activation process is the same as the initial sign-up process - once you have subscribed via Partners’ offers you will be sent an activation link via email where you need to create or log in to an existing DAZN account.

💳 Payment and Billing

Issues related to the monthly fee and billing discrepancies. If you notice any discrepancies in the payment of the monthly fee, contact Itaú customer support for assistance. They will help resolve any payment-related issues. We recommend reading the Combinaqui regulations found on this Itaú page - https://www.itau.com.br/combinaqui for updated information. ##### ⏸️ Suspension and Reactivation What happens when payment fails or is overdue and how to resume access. Access to Deezer Premium via Combinaqui depends on the monthly subscription payment. Itaú attempts to debit from the 5th day of each month. If this debit is unsuccessful, Itaú continues to try charging on subsequent business days throughout the month. If no successful attempt is confirmed by the last day of the month, the combo is suspended from the 1st day of the following month and Deezer Premium is automatically deactivated. The good news: your profile, playlists and favourite songs remain saved in your account. You do not lose anything. 1. 5th day of each month: Itaú tries to debit the monthly fee from your account. 2. Insufficient balance: new attempts are made on subsequent business days throughout the month. 3. Last day of the month without confirmed payment: your subscription enters suspension process. 4. 1st day of the following month: the combo is formally suspended and Deezer Premium is deactivated.

  • If payment occurs before the last day of the month, the benefit is normally renewed at the start of the following month.

  • If payment occurs after suspension is active (for example, on the 2nd day of the following month), reactivation only occurs on the 1st day of the subsequent month.

  • Ensure the current account linked to Combinaqui has sufficient balance from the 5th day of each month.

  • If you have an “Account Limit” (overdraft), also check its availability. The debit can use it.

  • Pay attention to communications from Itaú about overdue payments.

❌ Cancellation

All about cancelling Combinaqui or the Deezer Premium subscription. You will continue to have access to Deezer Premium until the end of the paid period. After that, your profile will be changed to Deezer Free. If you want to cancel your Deezer Premium subscription, as it is part of the Combinaqui benefits package, you must cancel this package at Itaú branches, through customer service centres or via the app. If you cancel your Combinaqui benefits package, your Deezer Premium subscription will end on the last day of the reference month. Your Deezer account will switch from Premium to Free, losing access to several features. However, your favourite songs remain saved in your Deezer account, provided you do not delete your Deezer account. In this case, after cancelling Combinaqui, wait until your Deezer account becomes free. Then you can subscribe to the plan that best suits you directly with Deezer via our app or website www.deezer.com. Who are Fnac-Darty and WeFix?

Fnac-Darty is a leading French retail group formed in 2016 through the merger of Fnac founded in 1954 and Darty founded in 1957, two well-known retail brands in France and Europe. It specializes in electronics, home appliances, books, and cultural products, operating across Europe with a strong presence in physical stores and online. Known for customer service, repair services, and sustainability initiatives, the group combines traditional retail with e-commerce to meet consumer needs. In 2018, Fnac-Darty acquired WeFix, a smartphone and tablet repair service. They operate through dedicated repair centres, kiosks in Fnac and Darty stores, and mobile technicians, offering convenience and fast service. The brand is also known for selling refurbished devices, promoting sustainability through the reuse and recycling of electronics. #### What kind of Deezer offers are available and where? Discover all the insights about Deezer’s collaboration with Pass Culture! We’ll give you the lowdown on what Pass Culture offers, how to get your account up and running, and tackle common questions and troubleshooting tips to keep your experience smooth. This partnership underscores Deezer’s commitment to making music more accessible to young audiences, allowing them to discover and enjoy a vast array of musical content seamlessly. #### Who are Pass Culture? Pass Culture is a government-backed initiative in France that gives young people aged 15 to 18, access to various cultural experiences, from concerts and theatre shows to books, music, and streaming services like Deezerwhere you can get a three-months Premium subscription. This collaboration aims to enrich the musical experience of young users by providing them with unlimited access to over 120 million tracks, podcasts, and exclusive content without any advertisements. Additionally, it’s a great way to explore and enjoy cultural activities at little to no cost. If you’re eligible, you receive credits to spend on cultural products and experiences, making entertainment and learning more accessible. Simply sign up on the Pass Culture app, check your available credit, and use it to discover and book cultural experiences near you or online! You can have Pass Culture app on your phone. Just download it from Google Play Store or Apple Store. Alternatively, you can access all the offers through the website. #### What kind of Deezer offers are available in Pass Culture and where? RTL+ Partnership with Deezer in Germany, Austria and Switzerland

This article provides you with the latest information on Deezer’s partnership with RTL+, including an overview of RTL+ and Deezer offers, the user journey, how to manage your account and a list of frequently asked questions and troubleshooting tips should you encounter a problem with your account. Jump to section: - Who are RTL+? - Deezer Plans - Premium/Family - How to register and get support - How to activate your Deezer account with your RTL+ credentials - FAQs

Who are RTL+?

RTL is a German free-to-air television channel and is the largest private television network in the country. On their RTL+ streaming service/multipurpose app, they provide Video, TV, Live Events, Music, Audiobooks, Podcasts and Magazines. Since 2022, RTL+ has partnered with Deezer through an API (application programming interface) integration that allowed RTL+ customers to stream music, and audiobooks through the RTL+ application, which uses an API to access Deezer’s catalogue of music and audiobooks as if the user had a Deezer paid subscription. From June, 3, 2025, users are being invited to migrate their access to the Deezer application, where they will have access to new features like Lyrics, Shaker, Quizzes, Song Catcher and much more that were not available in the RTL+ app. #### Deezer Plans - Premium/Family With Deezer Premium, enjoy music offline, create playlists, skip ads, and use fun features like SongCatcher, Lyrics, and Music Quizzes—all in one place. Deezer Family gives you all those Premium perks, but you can share them with up to 4 family members—each with their own profile or independent account, with an option to create profiles for kids too!! #### How to register and get support To benefit from Deezer’s partnership with RTL+, you will first need to register your account on https://plus.rtl.de/ - you will be asked to provide an e-mail address and password, and once you have verified your e-mail address, you will need to set up your personal information (name, gender, date of birth). Once this is complete, you will be issued a Customer Number (Kundennummer) which is a 10-digit number that identifies your account. If you already have an account with RTL+, you can simply sign in using your RTL+ credentials. You can also register/login using a single sign on (SSO) through your Amazon account if preferred. Once your account has been created/signed in to, you will be able to upgrade your free account to a subscribed account, which allows access to premium television shows. If you get the package Max or Family, then beside higher streaming quality (UHD), and more simultaneous streams, you get access to music and audiobooks, provided by Deezer, through the RTL+ application. If you choose the basic plans - Basic and Premium - you will not have access to Music and Audiobooks content. Starting from June, all users with Max or Family, will be invited to start using the Deezer application that is available on iOS, Android, Mac and Windows devices or through the web version on browsers Chrome, Safari, Edge and Mozilla. Once you are an RTL+ customer, you will need to contact RTL+ directly if you have a question or issue about the billing cycle, cancellations, refunds or any technical issue with the RTL+ app. But if the issue happens in the Deezer app, then Deezer Customer Support is here to help you. For support on RTL+ side, do this:  - You will need to provide your 10 digit customer number (Kundennummer) during your correspondence with RTL+ - The RTL+ Customer Care team are open between Monday and Friday - 9am to 7pm - They have a community page - https://community.plus.rtl.de/s/ where you can find commonly asked questions and create your own

For support on Deezer side, do this: - You will need to provide the user ID of your Deezer account - How to find it - We have support 7 days, with a Customer Care team supporting users from Monday to Sunday between 9am to 6pm - Alternatively, If you’re stuck, you can always ask the Deezer Community. Our global Superusers and Community Managers share their top troubleshooting tips. Post your opinion or vote for your favorite ideas here. Here you can find all options to get help - Deezer Contacts

Note: Deezer does not hold information about your RTL+ account, as the service is provided by RTL+ via an API and not via a Deezer customer database. Therefore, Deezer cannot help with RTL+ application, payment, subscription or account issues. You can find more details here - https://plus.rtl.de/musik #### How to activate your Deezer account with your RTL+ credentials  To migrate your account to Deezer, you just need to follow these quick steps. On iOS, Android devices and for PC Desktop app: 1. Open or download the Deezer app, available on the Apple Store, Google Play Store or Microsoft Store 2. Login by clicking on the RTL+ Logo 3. Enter your RTL+ credentials (Email + password) if you are not connected yet to the RTL+ app 4. You will be redirected to the Deezer homepage

On the Web: 1. Grab your RTL+ login details 2. Go to deezer.com 3. Log in by clicking on the RTL+ Logo 4. Enter your RTL+ credentials if you are not connected yet to the RTL+ app and your music will be there

Connect To Deezer With Your Car Entertainment System

With cars becoming more technological all the time, inbuilt speakers and entertainment systems can now connect to Deezer so you can stream your favorite tracks whenever you are on the road. Great news for that road trip you have planned with your besties! Better still, there are also devices you can connect your car to which will also allow you to listen to your music. Below are the most popular apps and entertainment systems - so get connecting for your next journey! Jump to section: - Android Auto - CarPlay - Automotive OS - Waze - FAQ

Android Auto

The Android Auto app (image: AndroidAuto.svg) allows you to access Flow, all your playlists, mixes, favorite albums and favorite tracks directly from your dashboard on a selection of compatible Android Auto cars and stereos. If your car is compatible, you can easily connect via a USB cable. Once you’ve set up Android Auto, it should start automatically when you plug in your device. Note: Your personalised Favourite Tracks cover is also displayed in the Library tabs of Android Auto and Waze, making it easier to recognise your music at a glance. Note: With Android 6.2.12, you can now listen to any podcast, radio and music you’ve favorited and recently played #### CarPlay CarPlay (image: CarPlay.png) lets you enjoy Deezer safely while you drive. Browse your music, podcasts and radio, or ask Siri to play your favourite songs—all from your car’s built-in display. With Deezer for iOS 8.23 or later, you can access your Library, including: - Favourite tracks - Albums - Playlists - Podcasts - Radio - Recently played content - Downloaded music - Flow and other personalised mixes

Discover more from your favourite artists

Jump to section: - How to log in to Automotive OS - Troubleshooting - FAQ

What is the difference between Automotive OS and Android Auto?: Android Auto is Google’s phone-to-dash technology, which basically takes your phone and displays it on your car’s display. However, Automotive OS is its own operating system, meaning your phone is not connected to the display and the Deezer Automotive OS app will exist separately to the mobile counterpart #### How to log in to Automotive OS 1. Select the Deezer app on your car’s in-built screen 2. Scan the QR code with your smartphone 3. On your smartphone, enter the code from your car screen

Note: You must have a Deezer Premium, Deezer Duo or Deezer Family account else you will not be able to use the app within your car #### Troubleshooting If you’re having problems, make sure your app and device software are up to date and you’re connected to the internet. Failing this, restart the app or even restart your car. Lastly, if all else fails, remove the Deezer app from your car and then reinstall it. #### FAQ For the moment it will not be available, but it will be available in a future version. Deezer On Waze

Take your music on the road with Deezer and Waze and listen to millions of tracks! There’s direct access to Flow, your favorite tracks, and your recently played playlists, and there is support for your Deezer Family account. Jump to section: - Requirements - How to set up Deezer on Waze - How to unlink Deezer from Waze - Need more help with Waze?

Requirements

Deezer on Waze (image: Waze.png) is available for all Deezer users worldwide wherever the Deezer and Waze apps are officially available. This includes but is not limited to: Brazil, Canada, Colombia, France, Italy, Malaysia, Mexico, Singapore, United States, and the United Kingdom. Note: Some limitations will apply for Deezer Free users. #### How to set up Deezer on Waze Firstly, make sure you have downloaded the Deezer mobile app and the Waze app. Then, follow the steps below to connect to Deezer. 1. Open the Waze up on your device 2. Select the Settings icon (image: Settings.png) to access the menu 3. Go to Audio Player 4. Under Available Apps, select Deezer 5. Enter a location to start navigating — you’ll now see the music icon in the top right corner 6. Select the music icon, select Deezer, and start playing music directly through Waze

Note: Make sure your mobile device is paired with your car’s speaker system. Consult your car manufacturer’s handbook for more information. Tip: Personalize your Favorite Tracks cover in the Deezer app and it will follow you to the Waze Library tab! Update your cover from your Deezer Library and spot your favorite music faster wherever you listen. #### How to unlink Deezer from Waze 1. Within the Waze app, select My Waze 2. Select Settings (image: Settings.png) 3. Select Audio player (image: WazeAudioOptions.svg) 4. Under Your apps, next to Deezer, select Disconnect

Need more help with Waze?

If you’re having any issues with your Waze app that aren’t to do with Deezer, visit Waze support.

Devices: Mobile, tablet, desktop

Add Deezer Widgets To Your iOS Device

Widgets are links or shortcuts to your favorite apps - Deezer widgets in particular, are a great way of accessing your music and changing your tracks easily and quickly. Widgets can be added to your device homescreen and are available in different sizes and types to help organize your music the way you like, so you can find what you are looking for super quick! Jump to section: - Availability - Deezer widgets that you can add to your device - How to add Deezer widgets - Related links - FAQ

Availability

Deezer widgets are available to all users worldwide. Widgets can be added in small, medium and large formats. You’ll need version 8.24 or higher of the Deezer app and a minimum version of iOS 18.5. #### Deezer widgets that you can add to your device You can add the following widgets to your iPhone, iPad or iOS device: - Widget: Description - SongCatcher: Open Deezer’s listening tool to help you capture, search, identify and add music - Flow: Your personalized soundtrack, which includes library and recommendations based on your musical taste - Your Library: Save time and open your Library with a tap - Made for you: You’ll find Flow and your Daily playlists, including smart track Family playlists - Recently played: The songs you’ve recently listened to on Deezer

How to add Deezer widgets

  1. Long press on your device’s screen
  2. Select the Edit icon at the top of your screen The Search Widgets screen will appear showing all the widgets available for the apps installed on your device
  3. Select Deezer from the list or use search bar and enter Deezer
  4. Swipe to see the available widgets and then select + Add Widget on the widget you want

Note: Alternatively, you can add a widget by long pressing on the widget and then dragging and dropping it in your desired position #### Related Links Using Apple ID with Deezer Deezer Siri Shortcuts Using Siri with Deezer #### FAQ To delete your Deezer widget, long press on your screen and tap the - icon. Alternatively, you can long press on the widget and a panel will appear with an option to remove the widget. Tip: To learn more about widgets, visit Apple Support here. Deezer values your feedback. Join the conversation at Deezer Community. Want more insight to the topics explored in this article? Find out more at Our own Deezer iOS 14 widgets are here!

Connect And Remove A Device

We all like connecting our music with our home or car devices to listen to our favorite tracks when doing tasks, housework or driving. With the Deezer Free, Deezer Student, Deezer Premium, Deezer Duo and Deezer HiFi plans, you can connect up to three different devices. However, you can only play music on one device at a time. With Deezer Family, you can connect and manage up to 13 different devices linked with six profiles. This means family users can listen and play music on up to six devices simultaneously. Jump to section: - How to add a new device to your Deezer account - How to remove a connected device - Supported devices - Not sure who is using your account?

How to add a new device to your Deezer account

To connect a new device to your Deezer account, simply install the Deezer app and log in! You can link up to three devices to your individual account. #### How to remove a connected device If you reach the limit of connected devices on your account, you can remove a device by following these steps: 1. Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application 2. Click on the Settings (image: Settings.png) cog in the top right hand side of your application 3. Select Offline & Data from the Preferences section 4. Under Download, select Manage my devices 5. Select the device you want to remove 6. Select Delete this device

Warning: This is irreversible. To link the device again, you will need to log in from that device Note: It is not possible to delete or unlink the device you are currently using from within the app. You’ll need to do this from the desktop app or deezer.com 1. Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application 2. Click on the Settings (image: Settings.png) cog in the top right hand side of your application 3. Select Devices (image: Devices.svg) 4. Select the device you want to remove 5. Select Delete this device

Warning: This is irreversible. To link the device again, you will need to log in from that device Note: It is not possible to delete or unlink the device you are currently using from within the app. You’ll need to do this from the desktop app or deezer.com 1. Go to your profile (image: Profile.png) 2. Select Account Settings 3. Select My connected devices from the tabs at the top 4. Select the device you want to remove 5. Select Unlink this device

Warning: This is irreversible. To link the device again, you will need to log in from that device #### Supported devices See a full list of all our supported devices and browsers. You’ll find everything you need to know about your device, including set-up and troubleshooting, in the sections below: Mobile, tablet, desktop Sound systems TV Cars Other devices #### Not sure who is using your account? Remove the suspicious device, but if you still think something is amiss, consider resetting your password.

Deezer Desktop App

Explore millions of songs in our catalogue with our Deezer desktop app. Simply download the desktop app for your computer so that you can stream music whilst working, playing games or browsing the web. You can access Flow, live radio and all of your favorite podcasts! Jump to section: - Requirements - How to download an album or playlist - How to switch to FLAC audio

Here are just a few of the perks of using the app: - Use hotkey control; play, pause, and skip tracks with your keyboard - Download content and listen in Offline Mode - Stream FLAC quality

Requirements

You will require: - MacOS 12 or later on Mac - Windows 10 (64-bit) or 11 or above

How to download an album or playlist

  1. Select the album or playlist that you want to download
  2. Select the Download (image: AudiobooksDownload.svg) icon to start downloading all of the songs on the album or playlist Once completed, a green tick will appear

Note: It is not possible to download individual songs #### How to switch to FLAC audio 1. On the player, select Audio (image: AudioDesktop.svg) 2. Select High Fidelity

FAQ

No, it’s not possible to choose where files will be stored. All files are stored on the drive that you have the app installed on. If you experience issues using the desktop app, we recommend updating the app to the latest version. Deezer values your feedback. Join the conversation at Deezer Community. Want more insight to the topics explored in this article? Find out more at Windows/Mac Deezer Desktop/Web App - Bugs/Issues/Workarounds

Listen To Deezer Offline

We all know connectivity to the internet or data is available nearly everywhere, but what about those times when you are stuck with no wifi or data? If you are a paid subscriber to a Deezer subscription, one of the many benefits is the ability to listen to your content offline! Jump to section: - How to download your content over WiFi - How to download your content via mobile data - How to view your downloaded music - How to turn on Offline Mode on your mobile - Helpful links

If you’re a Deezer Student, Deezer Premium,** Deezer Duo, Deezer Family, Deezer HiFi** or Deezer Family HiFi user, you can download your favorite content to listen to offline, including: - Albums - Audiobooks (select countries) - Favorite tracks - Playlists

Note: You can’t download tracks unless you add them to your library or to a playlist. You can’t download tracks on the Deezer website Tip: Learn more about creating playlists #### How to download your content over WiFi 1. Go to Library(image: image (14).png) 2. Select the respective section to view all of your favorite tracks, albums and playlists 3. Select the (image: Image 02-01-2024 at 14.06.jpeg) icon to make your content available for offline listening

  1. Go to Library(image: image (14).png)
  2. Select respective section to view all of your favorite tracks, albums and playlists
  3. Select the (image: Image 02-01-2024 at 14.06.jpeg) icon to make your content available for offline listening

Note: You can’t download tracks on the Deezer website 1. Go to Library(image: image (14).png) 2. Select the tab with the type of content you want to download. For example, Favorite Tracks, Playlists or Albums 3. Select the download icon (image: Slider.png) to make your content available for offline listening

How to download your content via mobile data

By default, the Deezer mobile app will only download your music when you are connected to Wi-Fi to save data. You can change this to allow downloading to happen when you’re using your mobile data. However, it’s worth checking with your mobile provider as downloading with your phone’s data can result in high charges. 1. Select your profile icon in the top left hand side of the application 2. In the top right hand side of the application, select the cog icon - this will open your Settings 3. Select Offline & data 4. Under Download, select Allow downloads using mobile data (image: Slider.png)

  1. Select your profile icon in the top left hand side of the application
  2. In the top right hand side of the application, select the cog icon - this will open your Settings
  3. Select Audio
  4. Under Download, select Download via mobile data (image: Slider.png)

How to view your downloaded music

To view your downloaded music; - Go to Library(image: image (14).png) - Scroll down until you see Downloaded music and select - A list of your downloaded content will appear

You will also see a section titled Download now, listen later which will use your algorithm to determine a list of tracks which you can add to your downloaded content for even more music offline! Tip: In your Library, you can switch between list view and grid view for your content. Tap the view toggle in the top-right corner to choose your preferred layout. Your view preference is saved separately for each Library section, such as Favourite Tracks, Playlists, and Albums. #### How to turn on Offline Mode on your mobile Offline mode allows you to listen to your downloaded content on your mobile without an internet connection. Perfect for travel or commuting, you can listen to all your favorite content offline and save data. 1. Select your profile icon in the top left hand side of the application 2. In the top right hand side of the application, select the cog icon - this will open your Settings 3. Select Offline & data 4. Under Offline mode, select Activate offline mode (image: Slider.png)

  1. Select your profile icon in the top left hand side of the application
  2. In the top right hand side of the application, select the cog icon - this will open your Settings
  3. Under the Preferences section, select Offline mode (image: Slider.png)

Siri Shortcuts For Deezer

All iPhone and iPod users know how beneficial the shortcuts function is, especially when it allows you to listen to your favorite tracks in seconds. With Siri Shortcuts, you can play your favorite track or playlist with a simple command. Shortcuts can be used to play Flow, your Library or Shuffle your music. Jump to section: - Requirements - How to create a shortcut - How to delete a shortcut - FAQ

Requirements

You must have a compatible iOS device running on iOS 14 or above. #### How to create a shortcut 1. Go to the Shortcuts app on your device 2. Open the Shortcuts app and select the + icon to create a new shortcut 3. A pop up will appear which allows you to Search Actions. Scroll down the list and select Deezer from available options 4. Choose the action you want to include in your shortcut. Available options from the Deezer app include: Flow 5. Favourite Tracks 6. Shuffle My Music 7. Like or Dislike a song 8. Once set, you can add the shortcut to Siri and activate it with a custom voice command 9. Select Done Note: You can modify the text for the command by selecting the + icon next to a previously added shortcut. A message will appear saying that you already have this shortcut added. Select OK. Then select Record Phrase to set up a new phrase to use the shortcut

How to delete a shortcut

  1. Within the My Shortcuts page, select Select
  2. Select the shortcuts you want to delete
  3. Select Delete
  4. Select Delete Shortcut

FAQ

If you are having trouble activating your Shortcuts, we recommend checking that your device can understand you and that you are connected to the internet.

Use Siri With Deezer

Got an iPhone and want hands free access to your music library on Deezer? Siri can play your Flow, library, or a specific song, album or genre of music to suit your mood. As long as you have iOS Version 13 (or later) installed, you can ask Siri any action without having to create your own shortcuts. Jump to section: - Requirements - Supported languages - Siri voice command requests - FAQ

Requirements

To use Siri with Deezer, you must: - Have a compatible iPhone or iPad with iOS 13 or higher installed - Ensure you’ve activated Siri on your device. Tip: See Apple Support if you’re not sure

  • Ensure you’ve downloaded the latest version of the Deezer app

Supported languages

We support: German, English (United States), English (United Kingdom), Arabic, Chinese, Danish, Spanish, Mexico, Finnish, French, Hebrew, Italian, Japanese, Dutch, Norwegian, Portuguese, Russian, Swedish, Thai, and Turkish #### Siri voice command requests Note: When using Siri Voice commands with Deezer Free accounts, you can only listen to music on Shuffle Mode. You can learn more about Deezer subscriptions here When using Siri voice commands, it’s important to start every command with “Hey Siri” and finish every command with “on Deezer”. - General requests: “Play some music on Deezer” - “Play Electronic music on Deezer” - “Play my Flow on Deezer” - “Play my favorite songs on Deezer” - Music on demand: “Play Beyonce on Deezer” - “Play the Homecoming album by Beyonce on Deezer” - “Play chill music on Deezer” - “Play my Sunday mood playlist on Deezer” - Playback controls: “Repeat this song/album/playlist” - “Repeat all songs” - “Stop Repeat mode” - “Enable Shuffle Mode” - “Turn off Shuffle” - Radio on demand: “Play radio Heart London on Deezer” - “Play Capital UK on Deezer” - Podcast on demand: “Play the podcast Intercepted on Deezer” - “Play the TED talks daily on Deezer” Note: It’s important to specify podcast, so Siri can find the correct content - “Play the latest episode of the podcast Serial on Deezer” - “Play the last episode of the podcast TED talks daily on Deezer” - “Play the episode ‘Meditation - Setting An Intention’ on Deezer” Note: Please ensure you are saying the exact title of the episode - “Play the next episode/previous episode” - Everything else: “Add this to my library” - “Add this to [name of playlist]” - “Find The Beatles on Deezer” - “Play the last episode of the podcast TED talks daily on Deezer” - “Find Beyonce’s songs on Deezer” - “Find a workout playlist on Deezer”

Note: When doing a search, Siri will open the app in the foreground and will not autoplay content #### FAQ Podcasts are currently available in selected countries: Algeria, Austria, Bahrain, Brazil, Colombia, Costa Rica, Egypt, El Salvador, France, Germany, Guatemala, Honduras, Iraq, Ireland, Israel, Jordan, Kuwait, Lebanon, Mexico, Morocco, Nicaragua, Netherlands, Oman, Panama, Qatar, Saudi Arabia, Switzerland, Tunisia, Turkey, UAE, United Kingdom, United States, and Yemen. Radio stations are currently available in selected countries: Brazil, Germany, France, Israel, Netherlands, Spain, South Africa, United States, and United Kingdom.

Deezer On Android Wear

With so many of us wearing smart watches these days, it’s really important that you can sync your favorite music with your wearable devices! WearOS 4 brings new features to enhance the Deezer experience with your Android watch. You can now download your favorite tracks and stream them from your watch without the need for your phone! Jump to section: - Requirements - How to log in on your watch - How to download content to your watch - How to play downloaded content - Still need help? - FAQ

Requirements

To use Deezer on your Android watch, you must have a Deezer Premium, Deezer Duo or Deezer Family account, a minimum version of 7.0.14.44 of the Deezer app, the WearOS 3 or 4 operating system on your watch, and an Android phone. Tip: You can find out how to download Deezer on your watch by visiting the Google Supportsite #### How to log in on your watch Note: Your watch must be connected to a cellular network or WiFi. Your Android phone must be nearby and you must be logged in on the Android Deezer app Tip: We recommend turning the Bluetooth on your phone off when downloading or playing content to improve the performance of your watch 1. Launch Deezer from your watch 2. Select Log in with phone You can now control music from your watch. You are able to access your Flow, Library, Recently Played, and all the music you have liked!

Alternatively: 1. Launch Deezer from your watch 2. Select Log in with a code. A code will be displayed on your watch 3. On the Deezer app on your phone, enter the code that is displayed on your watch

How to download content to your watch

  1. On your watch, select Offline mode
  2. Select Downloads
  3. Select the playlists you want to download (image: Checkbox.svg)
  4. Select Download

Note: To speed up your download, we recommend that you choose playlists that do not exceed 100 songs Note: We recommend that you turn off your phone’s Bluetooth when downloading or playing content to improve the performance of your watch #### How to play downloaded content You can stream your downloaded content without the need for your phone! 1. On your watch, select Offline mode 2. Select My library 3. Select any content that has a green tick (image: DownloadTick.svg) next to it

If you’re still having issues with Deezer on Android Wear, take a look at the Google help centre. #### FAQ - Samsung Galaxy Watch 4 - Samsung Galaxy Watch 4 Classic - Montblanc Summit 3 - Google Pixel Watch

This is something we are working on for future releases. This is something we are working on for future releases. For now, you can only download public and private playlists. You can still use WearOS 1, but we don’t have all the features that are detailed in this article. We don’t support WearOS 2. You are able to favorite tracks by selecting the heart (image: favourties.png) icon. However, there is no way to remove tracks from Flow via your watch. Deezer values your feedback. Join the conversation at Deezer Community. Want more insight to the topics explored in this article? Find out more at NEW Deezer app for WearOS3

Deezer On Apple Watch

With your Apple Watch, you can control your iOS device and the Deezer app. You can listen to your favorite tracks, editorial playlists, and personal playlists. With Share options, Flow, and improved speed, the Deezer app is easier than ever to navigate on your Apple Watch! Jump to section: - Requirements - How to download music on your Apple Watch - How to get Deezer on your Apple Watch - How to use Deezer on your Apple Watch - How to browse and play music on your Apple Watch - How to shuffle songs - Common issues and solutions - FAQ

(image: Deezer Apple Watch (1).jpg) #### Requirements To use Deezer on your Apple watch, you must: - Have the Deezer app downloaded for your iOS device - Have Bluetooth enabled and your iOS device paired with your Apple Watch

Note: Deezer on Apple Watch is an extension app and requires pairing with an iPhone via Bluetooth Note: Deezer Free users will have the download feature blocked #### How to download music on your Apple Watch You can download all your Deezer playlist content on your Apple Watch. This includes your Library, editorial playlists, and any personal playlists you’ve created. Note: To download music to your Apple Watch, you’ll need to use your mobile network or be on the same network as your watch. - If your phone is nearby, your phone’s network will automatically handle the download and transfer the data to your watch via Bluetooth or WiFi - If your phone is not nearby, in airplane mode, or turned off, the watch will only be able to handle the download if it is directly connected to the WiFi

Note: Depending on the space available and your Apple Watch model, you are able to download several gigabytes of data #### How to get Deezer on your Apple Watch Deezer on Apple Watch is an extension app and requires pairing with a compatible iOS device. 1. Set up your Apple Watch and pair it with your iOS device 2. On your iOS device, open the Apple Watch app and select the My Watch tab. 3. Apps that you can install will appear in the Available Apps section. Scroll down to the Deezer app and select Install Tip: On Apple Watch, make sure Show App (image: Slider.png) is turned on

How to use Deezer on your Apple Watch

To navigate and select options, you can use the Apple Watch’s touchscreen or its Digital Crown. From the app homepage, you can access the following: - Offline mode: Access your Downloaded music, including Favorite Tracks, Playlists, and editorial playlists - Flow: This tool plays your favorites and recommends songs that we think you’ll love based on the songs that you’ve liked Tip: You can learn about Flow here - Library: See your library of content and add tracks to it with the Add Library button

How to browse and play music on your Apple Watch

When browsing playlists, the most recently updated playlists will appear at the top. Note: Your playlists will be sorted according to your previous sorting option. For example, in manual order, A-Z, or by recently added songs. To begin music playback, choose a playlist or album and select on a song to begin. You can adjust the volume level using the Apple Watch’s Digital Crown. Note: You can also select the Dislike button to skip tracks and help Flow understand your music taste better #### How to shuffle songs To shuffle all the songs in your music library, go to Library(image: image (14).png)and then select the Shuffle (image: Shuffle.png) icon. To shuffle just the songs in a playlist, select the playlist you desire and then select the Shuffle (image: Shuffle.png) icon. #### Common issues and solutions We have compiled a list of common issues and their solutions: - For large playlists: You may have storage limitations on your device, so breaking them down into smaller playlists will ensure they play successfully - Ensure your headphones are paired directly with the watch in the watch’s Bluetooth settings - Make sure the content is fully downloaded before going offline. To listen to music on your Apple Watch without your phone, you’ll need to: Make sure your content is downloaded to the watch while connected to WiFi/network - Pair your Bluetooth headphones directly with your Apple Watch - Use the mode in the Deezer watch app to access your downloaded music. If you’re having trouble downloading, try creating smaller playlists or turning off Bluetooth on your iPhone during the download process for faster transfers

FAQ

Yes, downloaded content can be listened to without any network connection or your phone Deezer Family is accessible on Apple Watch. Every member of your family can own an Apple Watch and connect their iOS device. Note: You cannot switch Deezer Family accounts using Apple Watch. You’ll need to do so from your iPhone. Note: You can pair several watches to one iPhone. If you have multiple watches, be sure you’ve found and paired the correct watch when you are setting up the device. Deezer values your feedback. Join the conversation at Deezer Community. Want more insight to the topics explored in this article? Find out more at Our brand new Apple Watch app is here!

Deezer On Garmin

Everybody needs an upbeat playlist to guide them through the hardest of workouts. Now you can take all your Deezer music with you on your Garmin watch. Listen to your favorite tracks, enjoy Deezer’s catalog of millions of songs and explore Garmin’s bespoke playlists! Jump to section: - Requirements - Compatible devices - Content limits - How to download Deezer on your Garmin watch - How to connect your Deezer account to Garmin - How to redeem Garmin gift vouchers - How to unlink your Deezer account from Garmin - How to download music to your Garmin - Garmin playlists - Still need help? - FAQ

Requirements

To use Garmin with Deezer you must: - Be using Android 9.0 (image: GooglePlay.svg) or above - Be using iOS 18.5 (image: iOSAppStore.svg) or above, compatible with iPhone or iPod Touch - Have a Deezer Premium or Deezer Family subscription plan - Have downloaded the Garmin Connect (image: GarminConnect.jpg) app - Have Bluetooth headphones Tip: You can find out more information here - Have your Garmin watch set up to connect to WiFi - Ensure that your Garmin is updated to the latest software - for minimum requirements, please check here

Compatible devices

Garmin first included Deezer preloaded on its Forerunner 245 Music and Forerunner 945. All models produced after May 2019 now include Deezer preloaded and do not require users to download the app from the Garmin Connect Mobile store. Here is a list of some compatible devices: - fēnix® 5/5S/5X Plus (fēnix 5 Plus Series) - fēnix® 6 Pro/ Sapphire/6S Pro/6S Sapphire/6X Pro/6X Sapphire/6X Pro Sola - vivoactive 3 Music - vivoactive 4 or Venu Series - Legacy Saga series: Darth Vader and Rey - Legacy Heroes series: Captain Marvel and First Avenger - Venu - Forerunner 245 Music, 645 Music, and 945 - D2 Delta / S / PX - MARQ™ Adventurer/Athlete/Aviator/Captain/Captain: American Magic Edition/ Commander/Driver/Expedition/Expedition - tactix® Delta Sapphire

A full list of compatible devices can be found here. #### Content limits You can have 100-250 songs. Garmin has a 500MG storage cap for music on a device. At this time, it is not possible to upload your Deezer MP3’s to Garmin. Note: Playlists with more than 150 songs may result in problems syncing. If an error message appears notifying you that your device does not have sufficient storage space, please remove content to free up space Note: At this time, it is not possible to listen to live radio or podcasts using Deezer #### How to download Deezer on your Garmin watch 1. Open the Garmin Connect app on your smartphone and tap the hamburger menu located in the top-left corner Note: on iOS devices you will need to select More first 2. Select Connect IQ Store 3. Search for the Deezer app 4. Select the app and download

Note: If you’re having trouble setting up your watch, check that your device is connected to a charger and the battery isn’t running low. Turn your Garmin’s WiFi off and on and then try to sync again. If this doesn’t work, please contact Garmin support #### How to connect your Deezer account to Garmin The steps below are for Garmin smartwatches with physical buttons. Steps vary for Garmin smartwatches that use a touchscreen. 1. On your Garmin watch, after syncing, a message should appear informing you that a music provider has been added. Select OK to confirm 2. Go to Manage settings 3. Select Manage 4. Select Music Providers 5. Select Deezer You’ll be prompted to open the Garmin Connect app on your smartphone 6. Select Next 7. Follow the steps to sign in to your Deezer account

Note: Having multiple Garmin smartwatches paired to one account may cause issues with pairing and syncing. For steps on how to remove a Garmin device from the Garmin Connect app, click here Note: If you are a Deezer Family account holder, only the main profile and independent accounts of the Family account can be linked to a Garmin watch (sub-profiles are not supported). Note: Only one Deezer account can be linked to a Garmin watch at any one time #### How to redeem Garmin gift vouchers Once you’ve finished connecting your Deezer account with Garmin, you’ll also be notified if you’re eligible for any special offers. 1. Go to the special offer gift page 2. Enter the voucher code displayed on your Garmin Note: If you’re having trouble redeeming your offer, please double-check you have entered your voucher code correctly. If you are still having issues, contact us 3. Select Redeem 4. Finally, enter your payment details

Note: At the end of the period, you’ll automatically be charged for the monthly renewal of your subscription, unless you choose to cancel and revert back to Deezer Free #### How to unlink your Deezer account from Garmin 1. From your Garmin watch, go to Manage settings 2. Select Manage 3. Select Music Providers 4. Select Deezer 5. Select Options and then Log Out 6. Select Yes

Once you successfully unlink your Deezer account, all previously stored music will be removed from your device. #### How to download music to your Garmin If you’re new to Deezer, take a moment to add some songs to your Library and add some playlists to your account before you attempt to sync your Garmin 1. From your Garmin watch, Go to Manage settings 2. Select Manage 3. Select Music Providers 4. Select Deezer 5. Select My Playlists to choose playlists you’d like to download 6. Select START to select the playlists 7. Select UP until you reach Done and then select START 8. Select BACK until the Ready to sync? notification appears and confirm the sync

Note: If your Garmin stops syncing during the transfer process, it may be because the Wi-Fi connection has been lost - in that case, check the internet on your Garmin watch. Additionally, being closer to the Wi-Fi router when syncing can improve the process. #### Garmin playlists Here are some playlists from Garmin to get you started: - Why I run: solitude - Ride with Rusch - Self-care soundtrack

  • Deezer and Garmin Troubleshooting
  • Garmin Support
  • Deezer.community

FAQ

If your playlists disappear from your Garmin watch, it may be because your Deezer subscription has expired or you’ve exceeded your limit on offline devices. Make sure your Garmin is charged and in range of your Wi-Fi network to verify your account status at least once every 15 days. iOS users can get help here regarding Bluetooth permission errors.
