Add A Deezer Family Member To Your Sonos

Making sure everyone in your family can listen to music whenever they want is really important - Sonos agrees, which is why on Sonos you can add individual member profiles or accounts. With your Deezer Family plan, you can add Family member Profiles, including kid-profiles, to your Sonos following the steps below. Jump to section: - How to add a Family member to your Sonos - How to switch Family members

Note: Independent accounts can use their credentials to log in independently. You can learn more here Tip: Learn more about Sonos or setting up your Deezer Family account #### How to add a Family member to your Sonos 1. Within the Sonos app, from the Settings tab, select Services & Voice 2. Under Music & Content, select Add a new account 3. Select Add to Sonos and then select I have an account 4. Select Authorize and then enter the credentials of the Admin account 5. Select the Profile that you want to add to your Sonos and select Accept

Note: If this is not the right Family account, select Not you? and enter the new credentials 1. Within the Sonos app, under Your Services, click the + icon next to the Deezer logo to access Suggested Services 2. Select Deezer 3. Select + Add a New Account 4. Select I have an account to log in and authorise access 5. Select the Profile that you want to add to your Sonos

Note: If this is not the right Family account, select Not you? and enter the new credentials 1. Within the Sonos app, select Add Music Services 2. Select Deezer 3. Select I already have an account 4. Select Authorize 5. Enter the credentials of the Admin account 6. Select the Family Member Profile that you would like to connect

Note: If this is not the right Family account, select Not you? and enter the new credentials #### How to switch Family members 1. Go to Browse 2. Select Deezer 3. Select the account name to reveal a list of added Profiles 4. Select the Profile you want to use

  1. Go to Browse

  2. Select Deezer

  3. Select the account name to reveal a list of added Profiles

  4. Select the Profile you want to use

  5. Within the Select a Music Source panel, select the triangle (image: DropdownArrow.svg) to the right of the service name to reveal a list of added Profiles

  6. Select the account you want to use

Deezer And Sonos

Started to cook a family meal and want to listen to the latest new music? Or maybe you are having a romantic evening in with your new beau and want some background music to set the mood. The Sonos app allows you to stream your entire Deezer music library to any room in the house! You can also use Sonos voice control to play your favorite tracks in a single command. Jump to section: - Requirements - How to set up Deezer on your Sonos - How to remove Deezer from your Sonos - How to set up and use Sonos Voice Control - How to use Deezer with Amazon Alexa on Sonos - Helpful links - FAQ

Note: Sonos Voice Control is only available in the United States and France #### Requirements - You must have either a Deezer Premium, Deezer Duo or Deezer Family subscription

How to set up Deezer on your Sonos

  1. Open the Sonos app

  2. Select your Profile icon which is displayed in the top right corner

  3. Select Content Services

  4. Click on Add Content Service

  5. Select Deezer from the list

  6. Click on “I already have an account”

  7. Login to your Deezer account using your username and password combination

  8. Select your Deezer profile to link with Sonos

  9. Open the Sonos app

  10. Select your Profile icon which is displayed in the top right corner

  11. Select Content Services

  12. Click on Add Content Service

  13. Select Deezer from the list

  14. Click on “I already have an account”

  15. Login to your Deezer account using your username and password combination

  16. Select your Deezer profile to link with Sonos

  17. Within the Sonos app, select Add Music Services

  18. Select Deezer

  19. Select I already have an account

  20. Select AUTHORIZE

  21. Type in your username and password when prompted and then follow the instructions

How to remove Deezer from your Sonos

  1. Within the Sonos app, from the Settings tab, select Services & Voice
  2. Under Music & Content, select My Services
  3. Select Deezer
  4. Select Remove account to remove it from your Sonos system

Note: If you’ve added multiple accounts, make sure to select the correct one before removing 1. Within the Sonos app, from the Settings tab, select Services & Voice 2. Under Music & Content, select My Services 3. Select Deezer 4. Select Remove account to remove it from your Sonos system

Note: If you’ve added multiple accounts, make sure to select the correct one before removing 1. Within the Sonos app, select Manage 2. Select Service Settings 3. Next to Deezer, select Remove or - to remove it from your Sonos system

Note: If you’ve added multiple accounts, make sure to select the correct one before removing #### How to set up and use Sonos Voice Control Sonos Voice control allows you to control your Sonos with just your voice! Just like speaking to Alexa or Google devices, simply say “Hey Sonos, play Father John Misty” in this format, and you’ll be able to play anything. This works with your Library and Flow, too:  “Hey Sonos, play Flow Focus”, “Hey Sonos, play Flow Chill”! Skip a song with a simple, “Hey Sonos, next song”, or turn it up with a, “Hey Sonos, volume up”! Note: Sonos Voice Control is only available in the United States and France. You must be on a Deezer Premium, Deezer Duo or Deezer Family plan to use this feature To set up Sonos Voice Control, follow these steps: 1. Within the Sonos app, from the Settings tab, select Services & Voice 2. Select Sonos Voice Control 3. Select Add Product You will be taken on a short tour to explain how to use Voice Control 4. Select the speaker that you want Voice Control to be enabled for

Note: Under Sonos Voice Control Settings, the default service should be Deezer if that is how you set up your Sonos. If this is not set to Deezer, select it and then change it to Deezer #### How to use Deezer with Amazon Alexa on Sonos You can see how to set up Deezer on Amazon Alexa here and find out more about voice commands for Deezer on Amazon Alexa here. #### Helpful links Sonos Support Speaker not found Using AirPlay with Sonos #### FAQ High Fidelity audio is music encoded in FLAC (16-bit, 44.1kHz). Streaming usually compresses music a little to save you data, but Deezer HiFi audio plays CD-quality sound. Select Info & Options and then Track Format. 5 Mbps is the minimum broadband speed required to stream FLAC from Deezer on Sonos. If you are streaming different FLAC music to multiple speakers, we recommend a higher broadband speed. - 5.0 Megabits per second - 1 FLAC stream - 7.5 Megabits per second - 2 FLAC streams - 10 Megabits per second - 3 or more FLAC streams

When streaming lossless audio to a Sonos group that contains many players, your Sonos system’s capabilities may be more limited than usual. Occasionally, the system might experience audio dropouts or display error messages in the app. To improve the performance and reliability of your sound: - Ungroup some of your players to make a smaller group - Wire some of your Sonos players to the network via Ethernet for local bandwidth for the Sonos players to use when streaming lossless content

The majority of Deezer’s millions of songs are available in FLAC quality. However, if you find a song that does not play when you select it, this may be because it is not available in FLAC at the moment. Try the troubleshooting tips found here. If that doesn’t work, send Sonos an email using the webform. Read Sonos’ article on audio playback stops or skips. You can also watch Sonos’ video on Wireless interference for more tips. Sonos can only index a maximum of 1,000 playlists with up to 40,000 songs total. If you exceed this limit, some of your playlists or songs will not be visible in Imported Playlists. You’ll need to reduce large playlists by reorganizing or deleting them. Deezer values your feedback. Join the conversation at Deezer Community. Want more insight to the topics explored in this article? Find out more at Deezer x Sonos

Deezer On Amazon Alexa

Started to cook a family meal and want to listen to the latest new music? Or maybe you are having a romantic evening in with your new beau and want some background music to set the mood. Use your voice to play millions of tracks with Deezer on Amazon Alexa. Listen to Flow and enjoy expertly curated playlists without lifting a finger! Jump to section: - Requirements - Compatible devices - Available countries - Languages supported - How to link Deezer on the Alexa app - How to switch Deezer Family profiles on the Alexa App - How to make Deezer your default music service

Tip: Check out our article on voice commands here #### Requirements Deezer is available on all Amazon Echo devices. Deezer is also compatible with Alexa-enabled devices. You can search compatible speakers on Amazon.com. Remember to check with the manufacturer that Alexa and Deezer are supported. Note: High Fidelity audio (FLAC) streaming is not available for Deezer on Amazon Alexa #### Compatible devices There are a number of devices that Alexa is enabled for, including: Echo, Echo Dot, Echo Studio, Echo Show, Echo Spot, Echo Plus, Sonos One, Ultimate Ears BLAST and Ultimate Ears MEGABLAST #### Available countries Amazon Alexa is available in Australia, Brazil, Canada, France, Germany, Italy, Mexico, Spain, the United Kingdom, and the United States. #### Languages supported Amazon Alexa supports several different languages - you will to set your Alexa to one of the following: - German - English (AU) - English (CA) - English (UK/IE) - English (US) - Spanish - Spanish (MX) - Spanish (US) - French (CA) - French (FR) - Italian - Portuguese (BR)

Please visit Amazon’s support page for more information on how to change your language settings on your Alexa device. #### How to link Deezer on the Alexa App 1. Open the Alexa app on your device 2. Select More (☰) in the bottom of the page, then select Settings 3. Under Alexa Preferences, click Music & Podcasts 4. Select Link New Service 5. Select Deezer from the list 6. Log in to your Deezer account and choose the profile you want to link to Alexa

Note: In order to link your Deezer account to Alexa, you must ensure that you have an email registered with your Deezer account - without this, the link will not work Note: If you’re subscribed to Deezer Family, you’ll need to select your profile to connect #### How to switch Deezer Family profiles on the Alexa App To switch family profiles, disable the skill and relink your profile following these steps: 1. In the Amazon Alexa app, tap More (☰) 2. Select Skills & Games 3. Select the Search icon, and enter Deezer 4. Select Deezer 5. Select DISABLE SKILL 6. Select DISABLE 7. Select ENABLE TO USE 8. Log in to your Deezer account 9. Select the Member Profile you wish to link 10. Select Accept to grant permission

How to make Deezer your default music service

  1. Within the Amazon Alexa app, select More
  2. Select Settings
  3. Within Alexa Preferences, select Music & Podcasts
  4. Within Settings, select Default Services
  5. Select Change next to the relevant category and then select Deezer

Deezer on Amazon Alexa Voice Commands Help

With Deezer on Amazon Alexa, you can ask Alexa to play any music, your Flow, your library, your personal playlists, or a specific genre of music to suit your mood. Find inspiration with our Editor’s playlists, which are updated regularly with new and exciting tracks. Jump to section: - How to use Amazon Alexa voice commands on Deezer - Other Alexa commands - Language settings

Tip: Check out our Amazon Alexa article here, which details how to set up Alexa with Deezer #### How to use Amazon Alexa voice commands on Deezer When using Alexa, it’s important to start your commands with “Alexa” and, if Deezer is not your default music service, you’ll need to end every command with “on Deezer”. Here are some examples: - “Alexa, play Rumours by Fleetwood Mac on Deezer” - “Alexa, play Father John Misty on Deezer” - “Alexa, play [Deezer Editor’s playlist] on Deezer” - “Alexa, play my Flow on Deezer” - “Alexa, play Ska music on Deezer” - “Alexa, play my library on Deezer” - “Alexa, play [your personal playlist] on Deezer”

Tip: Learn how to make Deezer your default music service. #### Other Alexa Commands You can ask Alexa to do a lot more, like tell you what song is playing, skip to the next track, and change the volume level with these commands: - “Alexa, what’s playing?” - “Alexa, resume music” - “Alexa, next song” - “Alexa, I like this song” - “Alexa, I dislike this song” - “Alexa, stop the music” - “Alexa, set volume to 5”

Tip: For further voice commands for Alexa, visit the Amazon support site. #### Language settings Amazon Alexa supports several different languages - you will to set your Alexa to one of the following: - German - English (AU) - English (CA) - English (UK/IE) - English (US) - Spanish - Spanish (MX) - Spanish (US) - French (CA) - French (FR) - Italian - Portuguese (BR)

Please visit Amazon’s support page for more information on how to change your language settings on your Alexa device.

Follow the steps below to connect your Apple HomePod to your iPhone or iPad: - Plug in the HomePod - Place it on a stable surface with plenty of space, and wait for a chime and pulsing white light - Start setup: Unlock your iPhone/iPad and hold it near to the Homepod - Select Set Up when it appears on the screen

  • Centre the HomePod in your camera view or enter the passcode manually

  • Choose your settings (e.g.; Room location and Siri preferences)

  • The final HomePod settings continue in the app

For more information in connecting your HomePod, please click here. ##### How to Check if Deezer is Connected to Your HomePod Account - Open the Home app on your phone - Click on the three dot icon in the top right hand corner and select Home settings - Ensure that the HomePod email address is the one linked to your Apple HomePod/Home app - Click on your Home account - this is where your username or email is shown - Under Connected Media ensure that Deezer appears on the list

If Deezer is not listed, follow these steps: - Open your Deezer application - Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application - Select the Settings cog at the top right hand side of your application - Within the Preferences section, select Music - Scroll to the bottom and select Connect with HomePod

Once connected, go back to the Home app - Deezer should now appear under Connected Media. #### Siri voice commands To learn more about general requests and using Siri with Deezer, please see our dedicated article. With the HomePod you can: - Ask Siri to play a track, artist, album, playlist, or genre - Ask Siri to favorite a song or play Flow - Shuffle and repeat playlists and albums

Note: If Deezer is your default streaming service, it is not necessary to specify “with Deezer” at the end of every voice command #### FAQ Yes, both the admin and member accounts can play content. Make sure you’re logged in with the right account before linking your account with HomePod. Yes, HomePod supports HiFi quality. Apple HomePod will play the highest possible quality available. Requirements

Most Google Nest Audio devices are supported, including Google Mini and Google Max. However, we recommend checking your country and language availability. To set up your Google Nest, follow the steps outlined here. #### Available countries Google Nest Audio is currently available in the following countries: Australia, Canada, France, Germany, Italy, Mexico, Spain, the United Kingdom and the United States. Note: Google Nest Audio is supported in English, French, German, Italian and Spanish. Visit Google’s support page to learn more #### Voice commands If you’d like to know more about setting up voice commands, check out the voice commands for Google Nest Audio here. #### How to set up Deezer up on Google Home Make sure your Google Nest Audio device is set up and that you’ve downloaded the Google Home app from the Play Store. 1. Within the Google Home (image: GoogleHome.png) app, go to the Home tab 2. Select the add + icon located in the middle of the screen 3. Within the Manage Services section, select Music and audio 4. Select the link (image: Link.svg) icon under Deezer 5. Select LINK ACCOUNT 6. Enter your login details If you have a Deezer Family account, you’ll be asked to select your Member profile

How to switch Deezer Family profiles on Google Home

  1. Within the Google Home (image: GoogleHome.png) app, go to the Home tab
  2. Select the add + icon located in the middle of the screen
  3. Within the Manage Services section, select Music and audio
  4. Select No default provider and UNLINK under Deezer
  5. Select the link (image: Link.svg) icon under Deezer to relink
  6. Enter your login details again and select the correct sub-profile

How to make Deezer your default music service

  1. Within the Google Home (image: GoogleHome.png) app , go to your Account menu located in the right corner of the screen
  2. Select Settings
  3. Within the Services tab, select Music and audio
  4. Select Deezer

Note: This will be a radio (image: RadioButton.svg) button

Deezer on Android TV

With Deezer on Android TV, you can listen to your entire music collection on your TV. Jump to section: - Features - Availability - How to set up Deezer on your Android TV and Smart log in - FAQ

Availability

Deezer on Android TV is available to all Deezer users with Android TV version 6.0 or higher. Android TV is a Google TV operating system that provides access to the Google Play Store and is built into many TV brands, including: - Sony - Philips - TCL - Android boxes such as Xiaomi, Nvidia Shield et al - and many others

Note: The TV app is only available for Deezer Premium, Deezer Duo, Deezer Student and Deezer Family users ##### Fire TV (Coming Soon) Deezer will shortly become available on Fire TV, Fire Stick and other devices running Fire OS 6 or higher. Fire OS is a operating system developed by Amazon. It is based on Android technology but uses the Amazon Appstore instead of the Google Play Store. #### How to set up Deezer on your Android TV To download Deezer from the Google Play Store for Android TV, follow these steps: 1. Enter Deezer for Android TV in the Search bar 2. Select the app to install it 3. Launch the app 4. You can synchronize your Deezer account with your new TV app by scanning the QR code displayed on your TV with your mobile device and then entering the 4-digit code in the mobile application (login details will be required if you are not logged in on the app in your mobile phone)

Note: Make sure to update to the latest version of Android TV Tip: For further information about using your Android TV, visit the official support page If you can’t find the app in the Google App Store, you can find the new Deezer TV application on the bottom of this article. #### FAQ Unfortunately podcasts and radio are not available to listen to on Android TV. Deezer Freemium is no longer available on Android TV. To continue enjoying Deezer on your TV, a subscription is required. To see our plans, head to our plans page. Deezer Android TV app has been built to provide a smooth experience to all users, so we haven’t included all the same options you can find on the mobile application. However, you can switch profile in case you own a Deezer Family or Deezer Duo subscription and you can log in and out of the application. The default option is HiFi - so you can enjoy the best sound experience on your TV! Deezer on Android TV requires a paid subscription to use, so Deezer Premium, Deezer Duo, Deezer Family or Deezer Student. However, if you download the application and sign in, you will see a Subscribe to Deezer Premium QR code which will allow you to upgrade your account. If you have multiple profiles on your Deezer Family subscription and want to change between them, just select in your profile picture on the left bottom of the screen, select Switch account and click on the profile you want. If something is not working as expected, please follow the troubleshooting tips below: - Make sure you have a paid subscription or upgrade to a paid subscription - Deezer Free will not work - Switch off TV or Android Box and remove power cable for 30 seconds - Clear Cache under settings of Apps (from the menu of the TV or Android Box) - Clear Data under settings of Apps (from the menu of the TV or Android Box) - Uninstall, then reinstall the application

If these troubleshooting tips do not work, please get in touch with us and provide the following information: - Version of Android being used - Version of Deezer being used - Video or screenshot of the issue with a summary of the problem

  3. Go to Albums

  4. Select the audiobook album you want to add to your playlist

  5. Select the three dots menu at the top of the page

  7. Select the playlist you want

Deezer channels Deezer podcasts, radio & more… #### FAQ You can organize any podcast you’re listening to using the arrows to sort by: - Newest to oldest - Oldest to newest - Unheard - Duration

Yes. Deezer allows you to download podcasts for offline listening with an eligible subscription. To listen without using mobile data or Wi-Fi: 1. Download the podcast episode. 2. Enable Offline Mode in the Deezer app. 3. Play the downloaded episode.

Note: If Offline Mode is not enabled, Deezer may stream the podcast over mobile data or Wi-Fi instead of playing the downloaded version. Therefore, enabling Offline Mode is recommended to ensure your downloaded podcasts are played without consuming data. If you can’t find an individual podcast, it’s possible the show, station or author’s team have not added it to Deezer. You can request to add it here. Podcasts and radio stations are created and uploaded by external partners, and are not created by Deezer. Therefore, you may hear ads that the external partners have embedded into the podcast. Unfortunately, unless you are the podcast creator/owner, we cannot add a podcast suggestion without their permission. However, please ask the creator or owner to set up a Deezer 4 Creators account, and they can add their podcast to our collection by following the link here. Deezer values your feedback. Using Deezer: Features

Access Your Deezer Listening History

Are you in the car and Flow has just played a song you love? Maybe you’re at a party and love the last played track from a Mix and didn’t get a chance to find out what it was. Your Listening history is the last 100 songs you’ve played with Deezer. This comes in useful when finding new music! Sometimes you might not be able to favorite a particular song you’ve just listened to, or maybe you want to make a playlist because the last 100 songs were just too good! Jump to section: - How to access your listening history on web and desktop - How to make a “listening history” playlist - How to access your recently played songs on mobile

How to access your listening history on web and desktop

  1. Go to Library(image: image (14).png)
  2. Select Listening History Note: This may be located under the More (image: DropdownArrow.svg) dropdown due to your screen size

How to make a “listening history” playlist

  1. Select the (image: Checkbox.svg) checkbox at the top of your listening history. This will select all the songs in your listening history
  2. Select the three dots menu (image: ThreeDotsHorizontal.svg)
  3. Select Add to playlist
  4. Select New playlist and fill out the appropriate boxes

How to access your recently played songs on mobile app

Want your listening history on the Deezer app? You can see the last 100 things you’ve listened to in Just Heard. 1. Go to Library(image: image (14).png) 2. Select Playlists 3. Select Just Heard

Note: You can only add songs individually from your Just Heard to a playlist #### FAQ Resume Playback currently restores the track, but not the exact listening position/timestamp. This improvement is already being worked on. Support for exact timestamp recovery is currently under development for mobile, web, speakers, CarPlay, and Android Auto, with a planned release in the future before the end of 2026.

AI Content Tagging on Deezer

AI is everywhere right now, and it’s hard to avoid. Although AI tracks make up a very small proportion of our content repository, AI tracks are being uploaded to our platform, we want to be totally transparent to you and let you know when AI content appears on Deezer. In order to ensure you have the best experience when listening to your favorite content, we have implemented a visible pop up box that will appear highlighting that some tracks may be AI generated. Jump to section: - How do I know if I’m listening to AI content? - FAQ

How do I know if I’m listening to AI content?

AI content will only be suggested to you within albums, so it will be specific to an individual artist. If AI content has been uploaded, you will see the box below: Note: AI content is already excluded from your recommendations #### FAQ No - it is imperative that we are transparent with you regarding the content you are listening to, including if some of that content is AI generated. Yes - and we would really appreciate it if you could. You can report content by following the instructions in this article.

Change Your Country Recommendations On Deezer

With Deezer, you change the country of your content, meaning that you can listen to K-pop when you’re in France or Sertanejo when you’re in England. Our innovative Country selector allows you to change your country recommendations on your mobile or desktop to explore music across the globe. You can enjoy your local library, no matter where you are in the world and feel more connected to your home country. Concerts on Deezer

Finding concerts nearby can sometimes be time consuming and complicated, from making sure you don’t miss a concert of an artist you love, to ensuring you get the best value on your tickets. With the Concerts feature from Deezer, you can now find and get concert recommendations based on your music preferences and location all from within your application! Jump to section: - How to use Concerts

The Deezer app will now, on the homepage, feature a dedicated section that allows you to scroll through concerts from your favorite artists, determined by those you listen to most and offer you suggestions based either on your current location, or a location you manually select. All artists that have upcoming concerts will be presented to you with the most relevant concert for you based on your geographical location and a link to those dates. Selecting a date will transfer you from the app to a dedicated page of the concert you wish to attend, including the line up, a selection of songs to get you in the mood featuring artist content and a button to direct you to a third party ticket vendor, where you can purchase tickets! #### How to use Concerts Go to the Explore tab, and select Concerts from the Experience rail. Once selected, the page will display personalized local concerts based on your listening history underneath the header Around you. Select Concerts from the side bar tab at the left of the application. Once selected, the page will display personalized local concerts based on your listening history underneath the header Around you. Selecting a concert, which is listed by artist name and date, displays the event page which provides information about the concert including the event time, artist line up and importantly, the Get tickets button which when selected, directs you to a third party vendor. Going away for a mini break and want to see if there are any concerts? With Concerts, you can also choose to display concerts based on manually entering a specific location. For instance, if you live in Paris but are travelling to Lisbon for the weekend, you can manually search for events by entering the city name. You will also be able to see upcoming events from an artist’s individual homepage by scrolling down past their discography and selecting Artist name on tour. When selected, this will populate all the artist’s upcoming concerts, arranged chronologically by date with the location listed alongside. Clicking on an event will take you directly to the event page where you will be able to Get tickets. Deezer values your feedback. Join the conversation at Deezer Community. Want more insight to the topics explored in this article? Find out more at Go Beyond Streaming with CONCERTS!

It features millions of songs from our catalogue in addition to Flow, Moods and Genres, personalized Chill and Discovery playlists, and new release recommendations we think you’ll love - Explore: A place dedicated to our experiences and fresh, new music news, picked by our editorial team. Explore showcases concerts, podcasts, radio, audiobooks, music quizzes and Zen, editorial highlights of trending tracks, playlists and albums, editorial picks based on the season (festivals, summer, festive period and more) and categorization of all music genres - Concerts: Discover upcoming concert dates for artists displayed by your geographical location, which when selected directs you to third party ticketing sites - Library(image: image (14).png): Use the heart to favorite music tracks, podcasts, radio and audiobooks and save to your music library

Deezer Club

Jump to section: - What types of contests are available? - How do I access Deezer Club? - Discover exclusive experiences with Remix Lab - FAQ Access and eligibility - Participating in contests - Winners and draws - Prizes and tickets - Confusing messages and display issues - Trust and legitimacy - Other questions

(image: DeezerClub-banner for support page-720x272-1.png) Deezer Club is Deezer’s exclusive in-app feature that gives Premium subscribers in France the chance to win unique experiences with their favourite artists. From VIP meet-and-greets and exclusive listening sessions to concert tickets and festival passes, Deezer Club brings fans closer to the music they love. Read on to find out what Deezer Club is, how to enter contests, and answers to the most common questions. Note: Deezer Club is only available to users with a Premium, Duo, or Family plan in France. It is not available to Free users or Kid profiles. Note: Deezer Club is only accessible via the Deezer mobile app (iOS and Android). It is not available on the desktop app or web browser. #### What types of contests are available? Here are some of the contests organised by Deezer Club: - Listening session: Fans get the chance to attend an exclusive album listening session in the presence of the artist, with a VIP welcome and time to ask questions - Release party: Fans are invited to an event celebrating the launch of an album in the presence of the artist - Front-Row XP: Fans can attend soundchecks before a concert, watch the show, and enjoy a VIP reception and time with the artist - Shaker: Fans get the opportunity to share a Shaker session with their favourite artist - Concert tickets: Win tickets to see your favourite artists live - Festival tickets: Win passes to popular festivals

How do I access Deezer Club

Deezer Club is reserved for users with an active Premium, Duo, or Family plan. Contests are announced on social media and by email. Here is how to take part: 1. Go to the Club tab in the app and select Participer (Participate) 2. Read the contest information, then select Se connecter (Log in) 3. Enter your Deezer credentials, or log in using social authentication (Facebook, Google, or Apple) 4. Read the permissions page and select ‘Accepter’ (Accept) if you agree 5. Fill in your personal information and select Envoyer (Send) 6. A confirmation page will appear to confirm your entry was successful 7. Fingers crossed! If you are selected as a winner, you will be contacted directly by email with all the details and next steps

Discover exclusive experiences with Remix Lab

Deezer Club gives you access to exclusive experiences that bring you closer to your favourite artists. One of these experiences is Remix Lab, where you can create your own version of selected songs using official remix tools available directly in the Deezer app. Remix Lab is available during selected campaigns with participating artists. Your remix can be streamed, added to playlists, and shared with friends just like any other track on Deezer. Every remix is created with the artist’s permission, and all streams are attributed to the original work, ensuring artists are fairly compensated. Please note: Remix Lab is currently available in France only and may become available in more countries in the future. #### FAQ ##### Access and eligibility Deezer Club is only available on the Deezer mobile app (iOS and Android). Deezer Connect

⚠️ Deezer Connect has now reached End of Life This means the feature is no longer available and cannot be used to control your Deezer app remotely. We understand this may be disappointing and appreciate your understanding as we focus on improving overall app stability. You can still enjoy Deezer on a wide range of supported devices — see the full list here. With Deezer Connect, you can be in charge of music across different devices. Using your mobile, you are able to control the playback of music on the desktop app, other mobile devices connected with your account, or the Deezer website. Here’s how to use it! Jump to section: - How to use the Remote Control - Troubleshooting tips

How to use the remote control

  1. On your mobile device, open the player
  2. Select (image: AudioOptionsInPlayerMobile.svg) located in the bottom-left corner
  3. Select Deezer Connect
  4. Select a device Your music will play from the selected device

Note: To discover another device, it must be connected with the same Deezer account, it must be connected to the internet, and the app must be open #### Troubleshooting tips/strong We all experience problems with our technology! Here are a list of some troubleshooting tips to help you. - Make sure both your phone and computer are connected to the same Wi-Fi network. Deezer needs these devices on the same network to detect each other for casting. - Ensure you’re using the latest version of the Deezer app on your phone - Deezer uses Google Cast or Bluetooth for device pairing - therefore, make sure your computer supports casting (e.g., via Chrome browser or Cast-enabled apps) - Make sure you’re logged into the same Deezer account on both devices - Ensure that permissions are enabled on your Android device. To do this go to Settings, select Apps and then Deezer before selecting Permissions. This will ensure that permissions for local network/Bluetooth and location are enabled - Restart your devices - both your phone and computer, as rebooting can fix connectivity issues - Try the web player - go to www.deezer.com and log into your account. Once you have successfully logged in, check on your phone application whether it now recognises your computer under output options - You can use Bluetooth as a backup option if casting isn’t working - you can also connect your phone to your computer via Bluetooth and stream audio in this way

Deezer values your feedback. Join the conversation at Deezer Community. Want more insight to the topics explored in this article? Find out more at Let’s take control of your sound with Deezer Connect!

Deezer Home

Deezer’s Home tab is a personalized space with plenty of highlights and popular music releases to keep you inspired. Enjoy a dynamic listening experience perfectly adapted to the moods and moments of your day. Deezer’s Flow and smart playlists are updated on a daily basis to reflect your listening tastes. Jump to section: - Requirements - Flow - Your personal soundtrack - Launch a Mix inspired by a song you love - Deezer smart playlists - FAQ

Requirements

You’ll need an Android mobile or tablet that is running Android 6.1.15 or above or an iOS mobile or tablet running iOS 7.19 or above. Note: If you are a Deezer Free user, you’ll be unable to download songs from playlists. Kid profiles will not have access to some playlists due to age restrictions #### Flow - Your personal soundtrack (image: Deezer Music flow main image (1).jpg) Once you have favorited 16 tracks and/or artists, Flow will appear on your homepage; it’s all your favorite music, mixed with fresh recommendations and songs you forgot you loved. This feature uses an algorithm to get to know what you like and what you don’t like and plays an infinite stream of music chosen just for you. The more music you listen to, the better the recommendations will be. All you have to do is press the Flow button. Flow: - Is available to all users, including Deezer Free subscribers - Is located in the Home tab of your app and deezer.com - Is infinite! - Allows you to choose a Mood, which creates an infinite playlist based on that mood. For example, Chill or Workout. Just open Flow, select the Moods button and choose between Love, Workout, Chill, Sad, Focus, and Party - Allows you to pick a Genre which creates an infinite playlist based on the selection of that genre. For example, Rock, Pop, Classical etc. There are 32 genres you can select from!

Deezer Notification Center And Artists’ New Releases

Getting notified about the latest releases or tour dates from your favorite artists helps ensure you never miss important updates. Deezer offers different types of notifications, each working in a different way. Jump to section: - How Deezer notifications work - How to see your notifications in your Notification Center - FAQ

How Deezer notifications work

Deezer offers two types of notifications, and they work differently. ##### In-app Notification Centre The Notification Centre (accessible via the (image: NotificationBell.svg) in the Deezer app) displays personalized recommendations and updates based on your listening activity. These notifications are automatically generated based on your listening activity and music preferences and cannot be enabled or disabled. You may receive a notification about a new release when: - Deezer identifies you as a fan of the artist based on your listening habits - The artist is among your top 15 most-listened-to artists - You have added the artist, one of their albums, or one of their tracks to your Favorites

Push notifications and emails

Push notifications and email notifications can be managed through your Deezer notification preferences. To manage these notifications: 1. Go to Account Settings > Notifications. 2. Select or deselect the notifications you would like to receive.

To receive notifications about new releases from artists you follow, make sure Artists I Like is enabled in your Notification preferences page. When one of these artists releases new music, Deezer may send you an email or push notification. If you would also like to receive notifications on your mobile device, make sure Deezer notifications are enabled in your phone’s settings. Note: These settings only affect email and push notifications. They do not affect notifications displayed in the Deezer Notification Centre. For more information, visit our Notification Preferences article #### How to see your notifications in your Notification Center 1. Once you’ve favorited artists, select the notification bell (image: NotificationBell.svg) icon in the top-right of your screen to see a list of new releases from those artists. Notifications are limited to a maximum of 25.

FAQ

If you open your Notification Center and a message pops up prompting you to add more artists, this means you do not have enough artists favorited. You’ll need to add more artists to your library to start receiving new release notifications. Alternatively, you may need to check your device settings or enable notifications. No, it isn’t the same thing. By adding more artists, you’ll help support the artists you love. It also improves your recommendations and Flow. Favoriting artists not only helps improve your personal recommendations and Flow, it will also ensure you receive the latest news about them. Learn how to add an artist to your library. - Check your notification settings - Check that your artists are still favorited - Check your iOS or Android device settings

Deezer values your feedback. Join the conversation at Deezer Community. Want more insight to the topics explored in this article? Find out more at How do I manage my notification settings on Deezer?

Exclude An Artist Or Track From Deezer Recommendations

Not everyone likes the same music, even if two artists sound alike. There may be a song or an artist you really don’t want to be recommended when you are using Deezer, so we want to make sure that we’re recommending music you love. You have the ability to exclude any artist or track that you don’t like so that we won’t add them to your mixes. Jump to section: - Availability - How to exclude music on Deezer - How to manage your exclusions

Availability

This feature is available to all users on iOS, Android, and desktop. For users with a Deezer Premium, Deezer Duo or Deezer Family subscription plan, this feature will appear on Flow and Deezer mixes Note: This does not include Discovery, Chill and New releases #### How to exclude music on Deezer 1. When listening to Deezer mixes or Flow, from the player, select the unhappy emoji (image: Unhappy.svg) 2. Select Do not recommend this track (image: DontRecommendTrack.svg) or Do not recommend this artist (image: Don_tRecommendThisArtist.svg) The player will automatically skip to the next track in the mix

Tip: You can also Change mood after selecting the unhappy emoji (image: Unhappy.svg) #### How to manage your exclusions Note: This is only available on the desktop version of Deezer 1. Go to your profile (image: Profile.png) 2. Select Manage my exclusions 3. Select the Excluded artists tab or the Excluded tracks tab 4. You can add, change or delete exclusions from this section

Note: You can also manage your exclusions from the unhappy emoji (image: Unhappy.svg) menu

How to Customize Your Music Recommendations on Deezer

Take control of your music experience with Deezer’s improved recommendation settings. Learn how to manage your music preferences, dislike tracks you don’t want to hear, and customize your algorithm to get better recommendations. Find out how to make Deezer’s algorithm work better for you. Want to fine-tune your music recommendations? We’ve made it easier than ever to tell us what you love (and what you don’t). Here’s how to make sure your Deezer experience is perfectly tailored to your taste. Jump to section: - How to dislike Tracks or Artists - Managing Your Disliked Content - What Happens When You Dislike Something? - Tips for Better Recommendations

How to dislike Tracks or Artists

Don’t want to hear a particular song or artist in your recommendations? No problem! Here’s how to dislike them: - When playing a song, tap the heart dislike button in the player - Choose from two options: Don’t recommend this track - removes just this song - Don’t recommend this artist - removes all songs from this artist

(image: Recommend:don’t recommend.png) Tip 👉 Banned content will only be removed from recommendations - you can still find it by searching or in your playlists. #### Managing Your Disliked Content Want to see what you’ve banned or change your mind about something? Here’s how: - Open your Settings - Tap “Manage my dislikes” - You’ll see lists of all your disliked tracks and artists - Changed your mind? Tap the X next to it

(image: Manage Recommend.png) #### What Happens When You Dislike Something? When you dislike content, Deezer will: - Remove it from your Flow - Keep it out of your mixes and recommendations - Learn your preferences to suggest better alternatives

Good to know 💡 If you dislike several tracks from the same artist, we’ll ask if you want to dislike that artist completely. This helps us better understand your taste! #### Tips for Better Recommendations Want to get the most out of your personalized recommendations? Here are some easy ways to help fine-tune what you hear: ##### 💜 Engage with what you love The more you interact with content you enjoy, the better your recommendations will get! - Like tracks, albums, and artists you love - Create playlists and listen to them regularly - Explore new music — your activity helps shape your future suggestions

✨ Be selective with dislikes

Use dislikes carefully — they tell us what not to recommend. - Only dislike tracks you really don’t want to hear again - Skipping a track occasionally won’t impact your recommendations as much

🎯 Choose the right option

Different options send different signals: - Use “Don’t recommend this track” to block specific songs - Use “Don’t recommend this artist” to avoid music from an artist entirely

🔄 Review from time to time

Your music taste can evolve! - Check your disliked content occasionally - Remove dislikes if your preferences change

Remember ⚠️ Disliking content only affects recommendations. You can still find disliked tracks by searching or in your playlists.

Improve Your Deezer Flow And Play Flow’s Moods and Genres

Flow is your personalized soundtrack, which includes library and recommendations based on the content you’ve favorited on Deezer, including: - Favorite tracks from your Library(image: image (14).png) - Music you listen to from the Deezer catalog - The genres you listen to and artists you favorite

The more music you listen to, the more you’ll improve Flow and Deezer smart playlists, helping Deezer make better music recommendations for you on your Home tab. Jump to section: - How to improve Flow - How to tune your Flow - The Deezer Home tab - How to remove a song or artist from Flow - How to play Flow’s moods - How to play Flow’s genres - Helpful links - FAQ

Note: If you want to listen to only new recommendations, try our Monday discovery playlist found on the Home tab #### How to improve Flow The simplest way to improve your Flow is by adding more content to your Library and listening to music across the app. Whether it’s an album, an artist or a track, Flow absorbs it all. By telling us what tracks, albums and artists you love or dislike, Flow can make better recommendations to suit your musical taste. You can tell us what you like by using the Favorite button. You’ll see it beside every track, album, artist, and playlist. Whenever you favorite a track, that content is added to your Library(image: image (14).png). #### How to tune your Flow Want even more control over what plays in your Flow? With the Flow Tuner, you can add or remove entire genres from your Flow directly from the player — no need to open the settings menu! To tune your Flow on iOS or Android: 1. Start playing your Flow. 2. Tap the Tune icon below the player. 3. View all genres currently included in your Flow and toggle any genre on or off. 4. Add new genre suggestions recommended by Deezer. 5. Your preferences are saved instantly, so you can enjoy a Flow that better matches your taste.

Tip: Each Flow configuration (such as Discovery Flow or Favorites Flow) can be tuned separately. Tap the Flow wheel to switch between configurations. #### The Deezer Home tab We created the Home tab specifically to recommend more music that you’ll enjoy. In your Made for you section on your mobile device, you will find personalized playlists, inspired by your favorite artists, and new music specially tailored to your music taste. By adding songs and artists from Flow playlists to your Library, your Flow will become even better at serving your music needs. Tip: Read more about the Home tab here Note: On desktop, your Flow will be within the ‘Flow: play how you feel’ section #### How to remove a song or artist from Flow Just select the (image: Unhappy.svg) button and then select Don’t recommend this track (image: DontRecommendTrack.svg) or Don’t recommend this artist (image: Don_tRecommendThisArtist.svg) and that song or artist will not be replayed. This will help us to make better recommendations for you. In addition to this, if you are not feeling the vibe of the playlist Flow has curated, you can select “I want more discoveries” - this trains Flow to present more music that closely matches the content you have listened to recently. #### How to play Flow’s moods Flow’s moods is our way of allowing you to listen to music that emanates a certain mood. Now you can put Flow on when you’re partying, and it’ll play party songs infinitely in just one tap! We’ve broken Flow’s moods up into six moods that you can choose from: Love, Workout, Chill, Sad, Focus, Party. This will let you choose exactly what you want, with all the benefits of Flow intact. To play Flow with a mood: 1. Select Flow 2. Select the Moods icon 3. Select the mood you want to listen to from the mood wheel

Tip: To return to the normal Flow, select Flow from the middle of the mood wheel 1. Select Flow 2. Select the Moods icon 3. Select the mood you want to listen to from the mood wheel

Tip: To return to the normal Flow, select Flow. This is located on the far left of your ‘Flow: play how you feel’ section #### How to play Flow’s genres Flow is also curated to display soundtracks that can be selected based on a chosen genre. If you’re feeling a rock vibe, and want to listen to personalized music that fits that genre and you know you’ll love, you can select a soundtrack from the Flow by Genres wheel that is displayed on your homepage. With 32 different genres to listen to, you’ll always be able to find something to fit - six genres will be displayed on your wheel depending on the genres you listen to most, but can be changed at any time. To play Flow with a genre: 1. Select Flow 2. Select the Genres icon 3. Select the genre you want to listen to from the wheel

The icon next to PLAY indicates what order the songs will play in: - Consecutive (image: Play.svg): tracks are played one after another in the order tracks are displayed. This will apply to albums Note: The order tracks are displayed includes any sorting applied, such as Recently added - Shuffle (image: Shuffle.png): tracks are rearranged and played in a random order. This will apply to playlists and Library

Note: If you want to listen to playlists or Favorite tracks in consecutive order, you must select the first track in the list and then check that the Shuffle (image: Shuffle.png) icon is not activated within the player Note: Only paid subscribers can deactivate shuffle on all content on mobile. Deezer Free users can only deactivate shuffle on certain playlists. Music Quizzes on Deezer

Our music quizzes, with the brand new party mode (desktop only), are a fantastic and fun way to challenge your knowledge of music, compete with your friends and family and contend with other players in real-time to see who can come out on top! For premium users, you can also Cast our Music Quizzes to your TV and enjoy the interactive experience on a grand scale, or launch party mode on your desktop and connect via HDMI cable to display on your TV. Premium users can also invite anyone to try the quiz experience, by scanning the QR code on the TV, regardless of their streaming service, with a quick 2 step sign up (email and username) to join. Jump to section: - How to play a music quiz - How to share your results and challenge someone - FAQ

(image: COMMUNITY ARTICLE-1.png) The game is simple: select whether you want to play on your own (solo), as part of a group (multiplayer) or party mode. In multiplayer mode, you can compete with friends either together in the same room or with friends in any location. In party mode, all you need to do is fire up your Deezer app on your desktop/laptop, get everyone to scan the QR code and let the quiz begin! You will have 30 seconds to guess the name of the song or artist that is playing - the quicker you answer, the more points you will receive! Each quiz has 10 questions you’ll need to answer - you can select quizzes based on a wide variety of musical genres (rock, pop) from themes (80’s, 90’s, musicals), quizzes based on a specific artist and even choose quizzes generated from your playlists: there is a quiz for everyone! #### How to play a music quiz If you are playing Party mode, select the quiz you want to complete and get everyone who is playing to scan the QR code that appears, including yourself. Simply choose a quiz from our many available options, sorted by genres, artists, decades and more! From there, you can select to play in one of three modes; Solo, Multiplayer or Party; - Solo mode allows you to train your knowledge, beat your best score and challenge a friend - Multiplayer mode allows you to invite up to 100 friends/players to the quiz (via social media and direct link) you’ve selected and compete in real time to see who comes out on top - Party mode allows you and up to 100 friends to play in real time in the same location, by scanning the QR code that appears on your desktop/laptop

Note: To play multiplayer mode in the same location, one player needs to use the sound from their device whilst other players answer using their own devices simultaneously To begin the game once all participants have joined, the user that created the multiplayer room, known as the Master of the game, needs to kick the competition off by selecting Start the game. To launch a quiz generated from a playlist, select the … icon in the top right corner of the chosen playlist and select Launch a music quiz from the list of available options. This feature is available to Premium/Family/Duo subscribers and must contain at least 10 tracks within the playlist. You will have 30 seconds to select the name of the song played from a list of multiple choices - the quicker you answer, the more points you will score! #### How to share your results and challenge someone Note: This is only available in Solo mode After you have finished, the score of the game will be displayed along with your best score on that particular quiz which can be shared on social media and with your contacts. You will also be able to see where you are placed in a Leaderboard of other Deezer users. If you want to challenge a friend to your chosen quiz, once your score has been displayed select CHALLENGE A FRIEND and choose the method of sharing - a message will be sent to each person selected. Note: Select Friends to see how you’re ranked compared to your friends! #### FAQ Only Premium/Family/Duo subscribers can launch a quiz in multiplayer mode, but all free and subscribed users can join a room to compete. Free users in solo mode will only be able to access one game. If you have a friend who is using a different streaming service, they can try the quizzes out for free using a 2 step sign in process (email, username), but the second time they try to compete they will need to setup a Deezer account to participate. Yes! This feature is available for Premium/Family/Duo subscribers, and the playlist must contain at least 10 tracks. No, this is an online feature available in mobile and web desktop. The quicker you answer, the better you score. The maximum score you can receive is 100, and for each 0.1 second that passes before answering, your score will decrease. Up to 100 users. My Deezer Month: Your Monthly Music Journey

Get ready to dive into your personalized monthly music stats with My Deezer Month! This feature gives you fascinating insights into your listening habits, helping you discover more about your music taste and sharing your musical journey with friends. #### What is My Deezer Month? My Deezer Month is your personalized monthly music recap that shows detailed statistics about your listening habits on Deezer. It’s a great way to track your musical journey and see how your taste evolves over time. ##### What stats can you see? - Total listening time - Number of artists you’ve listened to - Your top 10 artists - Number of tracks played - Your top 10 tracks - Your top 3 most-played genres - Your “Artist Crush” (newly discovered favorite)

💡 What’s an “Artist Crush”? Your Artist Crush is the newly discovered artist you’ve streamed the most during the month. These are artists you’ve listened to for the first time this month! #### How to Access My Deezer Month You can find your monthly stats in two ways: - Through your profile section

  • Via the dedicated carousel on your homepage

(image: Profile.png) ⚠️ Eligibility Requirement To access My Deezer Month, you need to stream at least 15 minutes of music before the last day of the month. Note: My Deezer Month is available only on mobile app (IOS or Android). #### Sharing Your Stats My Deezer Month is designed to be shareable! Show off your music taste and compare stats with friends. It’s a great way to discover new music through what your friends are listening to. 💡 Your stats are updated at the beginning of each month, giving you a fresh look at your previous month’s listening habits. #### Tips for Getting the Most Out of My Deezer Month - Stream regularly to ensure you meet the minimum requirement - Explore new artists to discover potential new “Artist Crushes” - Share your stats with friends to spark music conversations - Check back monthly to track how your music taste evolves

Remember, My Deezer Month is your personal music story - use it to explore new music, rediscover old library, and share your musical journey with others! #### FAQ Any track that you stream for more than 30 seconds counts towards your monthly stats, whether you’re listening online or offline. Yes! As long as your app reconnects to the internet within a few days, your offline plays will be synced and included in your monthly recap. If you can’t see your My Deezer Month stats, you might not have met the minimum listening requirement of 15 minutes for that month. Keep streaming to unlock your stats for the next month! This can happen if: - You had shared listening sessions - You used a connected speaker - Songs played automatically through playlists or autoplay - Someone else accessed your account.

Remember that any song played for more than 30 seconds counts in your listening data. No, My Deezer Month provides an automated, unfiltered reflection of your listening history. If you notice something significantly incorrect, please contact our support team for help.

The lyrics’ language is incorrect 6. The lyrics’ translation is incorrect 7. Select Submit A message will be displayed to confirm your submission

How to share a song to Instagram Stories and Snapchat

Sharing your favorite songs couldn’t be simpler. (image: Deezer How to Share Music youtube video-1 (1).jpg) 1. Select the three dots (image: ThreeDotsHorizontal.svg) button next to a track 2. Select Share

  1. Alternatively, within the player, select (image: ShareiOS.svg)

Quick Access on Deezer

When you open your Deezer app, you want to listen to your favorite music straight away, right? Nobody wants to spend time searching through their favorite artists or playlists to get the right vibe. That’s why Deezer have introduced Quick Access, a feature that allows you to customize your Homepage and have at your fingertips, the content you want to listen to as soon as you open your Deezer app. Jump to section: - How does Quick Access work? - How do I add content to Quick Access? - How do I remove content from Quick Access? - FAQ’s

How does Quick Access work?

You can select tracks, artists, albums, your Flow, playlists, podcasts and radio stations to “pin” to your homepage, making it easier for you to access the music you love. All you have to do once you’ve done this is click on the image and you’ll be taken straight to the content you’ve selected to pin! #### How do I add content to Quick Access? It’s really simple! Follow the steps below to get started. ##### via Long press To long press hold down your finger on the screen whilst the Deezer app is open. A pop up will be displayed on your screen that shows the Quick Access feature, where you can customize your pins by moving the visibility of content, or adding new music. ##### Add from album/artist/playlist page If you want to add content to your homepage, select the ellipses at the top right hand side on the cover image that is displayed when viewing the content (for example, a playlist cover, an album cover, an artist cover image). This will open up a menu of options to select from, including Pin to homepage. Click on this to add to your Quick Access banner on the homepage. ##### via Settings You can also access Quick Access and your pins from the Settings menu. Navigate to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application, and select the Settings cog icon at the top right hand side. Under Display, you will see an option for Homepage pins. #### How do I remove content from Quick Access? There are three ways you can remove content from Quick Access: - Use the long press function and select Customize your pins - Go directly to the track, album, artist, playlist and podcast by clicking on the ellipses and selecting Unpin from the homepage - You can also remove pinned content by navigating to your profile icon at the top left hand side of your application, and selecting the Settings cog icon at the top right hand side. Under Display, you will see an option for Homepage pins

FAQ’s

Yes - you can have a maximum of 5 pins on mobile, and 6 on desktop. Yes! You can add the following content - Tracks, artist, album, your Flow, playlists, podcasts and radio stations. Yes! You can pin your smart playlists - including Discovery, New Releases and your daily smart playlists - directly to your homepage for instant access. Just navigate to the smart playlist, tap the three-dot menu and select ‘Pin to homepage’.

Shaker on Deezer

Music brings people together and experiencing it with friends elevates shared moments with loved ones. But it isn’t always easy to get the vibe right — people have different tastes, their music libraries are separated on different platforms and crafting the ideal soundtrack takes time and energy. That’s why we created Shaker! Shaker also comes with the additional benefit of allowing you and your friends to play tracks from your Shaker group from any streaming platform. We understand that everyone has app preferences, so if your friends are using a different music app they can listen to your group’s content wherever they choose, and you can still invite them to your Shaker group and import their music from different platforms easily and quickly. Jump to section: - How to access Shaker - How to create a group - How to add tracks to group playlist - How to use Shaker as a non-Deezer user - Mix Tab - Fun facts Tab - Playlist Tab - FAQs

How to access Shaker

There are three ways you can access Shaker from within your app or on the web. From inside the Deezer application: 1. From the Home tab, select Go beyond streaming and select Shaker 2. From Explore, scroll to the top module on this tab, where we gather all our Experiences and navigate to Shaker

On the web: - Go to shaker.deezer.com to access Shaker on the web

How to create a group

To start creating your playlist, you will need to create a group first. To do so; 1. Navigate to Shaker 2. Select Create a group 3. Choose a name for your group 4. Select Share link to invite your friends

Once you have created your group, it will automatically appear within your Shaker group page and a mix will automatically be generated using all group members libraries, trying to identify and highlight musical compatibilities. Note:A group can have up to 15 members, so you can invite up to 14 of your friends! #### How to add tracks to the group playlist Once a group is created and includes a minimum of one additional member, a mix will automatically generate using a selection of group members libraries. You can also create a shared playlist. To do this; 1. Select the group you wish to add tracks to 2. Select Add tracks to begin curating your playlist

For more information about the different features of your group playlists, click to read Mix, Fun facts or Playlist Tab. #### How to use Shaker as a non-Deezer user If you are new to Shaker, and this is the first time you have been invited by a friend to collaborate on a Shaker group, you will need to complete the following steps: 1. Click the link received from your friend to join their Shaker group OR go to shaker.deezer.com 2. Follow the steps to create your Shaker profile 3. You will be asked to import your existing music library from another streaming platform. To do this, you will need to connect to the respective platform before the import can begin 4. Once the import has begun, it will complete in the background. In the meantime, you can enjoy the mix, discover sharable fun insights such as compatibility scores between you and your friends, as well as being able to invite more people to enjoy the Shaker experience!

Note: For a list of recognized streaming platforms, click here #### Mix Tab Once you and your friends are collaborating your music together, a unique mix curated by Shaker will be available to listen to. A mix is a selection of your group’s favorite tracks, which can be played across alternative streaming platform if preferred (Spotify, Apple Music and YouTube Music). You can also choose mixes that are mood specific, such as Party and Chill playlists! #### Fun facts Tab The Fun facts tab will display the musical compatibility between you and your friends based on your shared favorites, given as a percentage. Viewing a friend’s profile in this section will open up a personalized compatibility score and will present tracks and artists recommended to you based on your shared favorites. The stats tab also displays daily Fun facts that offer facts about your group’s musical tastes! #### Playlist Tab A playlist is where you and your friends can add specific tracks to listen to together, across preferred alternate platforms if required (Spotify, Apple Music, YouTube Music). These tracks can be from you or your friends’ Deezer libraries, tracks that you can select from the curated chart lists, and tracks that are suggested to you by Deezer based on you and your friends’ favorite tracks. #### FAQ Once a group is created, up to 14 other people can join, so invite away! No, it is not necessary for your friends to have a Deezer account or app to participate in Shaker. Regardless of the streaming platform your friends use, everyone can access Shaker and join in building the mix, creating playlists and discovering your music compatibility score. Shaker is an inclusive experience where everyone is welcome! Group content can be played through Spotify, Apple Music and YouTube Music You can share your Shaker link with all your friends through usual social media channels, SMS, email and WhatsApp. Use the built-in sharing feature on your phone or computer, regardless of the applications you use. Shaker follows the musical evolution of Deezer users. However, if you come from another platform, your music library will only update if you perform a new import You can find all your Shaker groups on the web at shaker.deezer.com or on the Deezer app through the Shaker brick in the Home under Go beyond streaming. You can also find it in the first section that gathers all Deezer experiences under the Explore tab To share your compatibility with friends, you can either share all the cards from the daily story using the Share function, or you can take a screenshot of your compatibility score with a specific group member and post to your social media account. Don’t forget to use the hashtag #Shaker!

SongCatcher on Deezer

There is nothing worse than being out and about and hearing a song that you love but don’t know the name of! With SongCatcher you can find the name of any song that’s playing. The tool allows users to quickly identify and save music that’s playing without leaving the app. You can even hum or sing the melody of a song that is stuck in your head and we will use our Humming feature to identify the song! We’ll give you the name of the song and the artist, giving you the option to add it straight to your favorite tracks or library if a match is found in the Deezer catalogue. Jump to section: - Features - How to quickly access SongCatcher - How to use SongCatcher - How to use SongCatcher’s Humming feature - FAQ

(image: Deezer Songcatcher promo image (1).jpg) #### Features With SongCatcher, you can: - Identify tracks playing around you - Hum or sing a track stuck in your head - Add songs straight to your Deezer favorite tracks or playlists - Discover new music

How to quickly access SongCatcher

You can access SongCatcher without having to go into the Deezer app. On iOS you are able to download the SongCatcher widget to find all the songs you want! On iOS and Android, if you press and hold the Deezer app logo, you can select SongCatcher from the menu to be taken straight to the SongCatcher homepage. Tip: Visit the article we wrote on iOS widgets here #### How to use SongCatcher (image: Deezer Songcatcher promo youtube video-1 (1).jpg) 1. Within the Deezer app, go to Search (image: Search.svg) 2. Within SongCatcher, select the SongCatcher (image: Songcatcher.svg) button SongCatcher will begin listening to identify a song 3. If it is successful, it will display the song and artist. From this point, you can save the song to your library as you usually would

How to use SongCatcher’s Humming feature

  1. Within the Deezer app, go to Search (image: Search.svg)
  2. Within SongCatcher, select the SongCatcher (image: Songcatcher.svg) button
  3. Select Sing now
  4. Hum or sing the song that is stuck in your head
  5. If it is successful, it will display the song and artist. From this point, you can save the song to your library as you usually would

Note: To go back to SongCatcher without humming, select Switch (image: Deezer Songcatcher additional youtube video (1).jpg) #### FAQ Yes, it’s available to all Deezer users worldwide. You’ll need the latest version of the Deezer mobile app for Android version 6 or higher or iOS version 7.11 or higher. If you do not see the SongCatcher tool, you may need to update to the latest version of the app. See our troubleshooting guide for Android and iOS devices. If the tool is unable to identify a song, it’s possible you are not connected to the internet. It could also be that the environment you’re in is too noisy or your device may be too far away from the music source. The last songs that have been found using SongCatcher will appear in Recent searches below the SongCatcher feature. You could even create a playlist and add all those songs to it! Tip: It’s best to capture the listening result and immediately add the song to your favorite tracks or to a playlist to save it. Deezer values your feedback. Join the conversation at Deezer Community. Want more insight to the topics explored in this article? Find out more at Humming feature, sing that melody in your head! and Have you checked our Songcatcher? and SongCatcher is now available for iOS users

Universal music sharing on Deezer

Deezer’s exciting Universal Music Sharing feature lets you share your favorite tracks with anyone, regardless of which music service they use. Friends and family on Spotify, YouTube Music, or Apple Music can enjoy full tracks without needing a Deezer account. Music sharing just got a whole lot friendlier across the digital streaming universe! Jump to section: - How it works - FAQ

Ever wanted to send a fantastic track to someone who doesn’t use Deezer? Now you can! Our Universal music sharing feature breaks down the walls between different streaming platforms. - Share Across Platforms: Send Deezer tracks and playlists to friends using Spotify, YouTube Music and Apple Music - No Account Needed: Your friends don’t need to sign up for Deezer to enjoy your recommendations - Full Songs, Not Samples: They’ll hear the complete track, not just a preview - Super Simple Sharing: Just a couple of taps and you’re spreading musical joy!

How it works

(image: 2025_STDA-16337_UniversalSharing_640x300_EN.gif) - Find that perfect track or playlist you’re dying to share - Tap the share option (image: ShareiOS.svg) - Pick how you want to send it—WhatsApp, social media, whatever works! - When your friend gets the link, they can choose their own music service they want to use - The track opens right up in their app, it’s that easy!

Music connects us all, and now your Spotify-loving friends can enjoy your Deezer discoveries instantly. Share those “You HAVE to hear this!” moments with anyone, regardless of their preferred music app. No switching services, no new accounts just great music flowing freely between friends. #### FAQ Most of the popular ones are covered! Spotify, YouTube Music and Apple Music are the platforms you can currently share music with Yep! Make sure you’ve got the latest version of Deezer to unlock this awesome feature Nope! They’ll just get the track you’ve shared, not a peek at your profile or personal information It happens sometimes! If a track isn’t available, your friend will see a message with some alternative options Share away! There’s no cap on spreading musical happiness with Universal Music Sharing Just music tracks for now! We’re focusing on getting the tunes flowing across platforms first Absolutely! Though keep in mind that some tracks might not be available in certain countries due to licensing (boring but true) The quality will depend on your friend’s streaming service and their subscription level, not on Deezer We don’t track that information so you’ll have to rely on the old-fashioned method of asking, “So… what did you think?” 😉

Select Create a playlist… (image: CreatePlaylist.svg) 4. Select the camera icon (image: CameraMobile.svg) if you’d like to take a photo or upload an image 5. In the Name field, enter a name for your playlist 6. If you would like your playlist to be Collaborative, select the slider (image: Slider.png) 7. Next to Private, select the slider (image: Slider.png) to make your playlist private 8. Select CREATE

If you would like your playlist to be Collaborative, select the slider (image: Slider.png) 7. Next to Private, select the slider (image: Slider.png) to make your playlist private 8. Select CREATE

Note: A collaborative playlist can only be set as public #### How to make your playlist Private By default, playlists that you create will be public. When you make playlists, any Deezer user can access them unless you change the privacy settings. If you do not wish for other users to view or add to your playlist, simply select the Private slider on your playlist creator or editor. Both public and collaborative playlists are searchable and viewable for all Deezer users. #### Collaborative Playlist on Deezer A Collaborative Playlist lets you and other Deezer users add songs to the same playlist. It’s a great way to build a shared music collection with friends, family, or anyone who loves the same tunes as you! ##### How to Create a Collaborative Playlist 1. Go to your Library(image: image (14).png)and select Playlists. 2. Choose the playlist you want to make collaborative or create a new one. 3. Select Edit 4. Toggle the Collaborative switch to on (image: Slider.png) 5. Hit Save – and now, anyone with the playlist link can add tracks!

Note: Collaborative playlists are always public anyone with the link can view them. Only the playlist owner can remove tracks. #### How to launch a music quiz from your playlist By using the tracks added, Deezer can create a music quiz based on your playlist! To do so, select the options icon next to your chosen playlist and from the list of actions, select Launch a music quiz. For more information about music quizzes, click here #### How to edit a playlist 1. Go to Library(image: image (14).png) 2. Select Playlists 3. Select the playlist that you want to edit 4. Select Edit (image: EditMobile.svg) 5. From here, you can: Change the playlist name 6. Set your playlist to be Collaborative with the slider (image: Slider.png) 7. Select the Private slider (image: Slider.png) to make your playlist private 8. Hold and the (image: MoveAndDrag.svg) icon next to a track to rearrange its position in the playlist 9. Select SAVE

Note: You can change collaborative playlist settings on deezer.com 1. Go to Library(image: image (14).png) 2. Select Playlists 3. Select the playlist that you want to edit 4. Select Edit (image: EditMobile.svg) 5. From here, you can: Change the playlist name 6. Select the Private slider (image: Slider.png) to make your playlist private 7. Select the Collaborative slider (image: Slider.png) to make your playlist collaborative 8. Hold and the (image: MoveAndDrag.svg) icon next to a track to rearrange its position in the playlist 9. Select SAVE

Note: A collaborative playlist can only be set as public 1. Under Library(image: image (14).png), select Playlists 2. Select the playlist that you want to edit Tip: To reorder tracks, hold and drag a track to the required position 3. Select Edit (image: Edit.svg) 4. From here, you can: Change the playlist name 5. Select the Private slider (image: Slider.png) to make your playlist private 6. Select the Collaborative slider (image: Slider.png) to make your playlist collaborative 7. Edit the description 8. How to customize a playlist cover

Make your playlists stand out by personalizing your covers with Deezer’s customization tools. Give your playlists a unique look by adding custom covers with stickers, shapes, and masks. This can help you easily identify your playlists and at the same time express your creativity! How to customize a playlist cover: 1. Open the playlist you want to customize 2. Tap the playlist cover image 3. Select Customize Cover 4. Choose from the available options: Add stickers 5. Apply shapes 6. Use masks 7. Upload your own image 8. Adjust your design elements as needed 9. Select Save to apply your custom cover

Note: Your custom cover will be visible to anyone you share the playlist with, making it easy for friends to recognize your curated collections #### How to add a song to a playlist It’s really easy to add a track to a playlist! Note: The playlists that you have edited or added to most recently will be displayed in the three dots menu If you are in the Player itself, next to the hearts icons select the + icon to add to a playlist directly.   2. Under Music, select Playlists

  3. Select the playlist that you want to edit

  4. Select Edit (image: EditMobile.svg)

  5. Swipe the track to the left to remove it Note: A bin icon will appear as you remove the track

  6. Under Library(image: image (14).png), select Playlists

  7. Select the playlist that you want to edit

  8. Select the checkbox (image: Checkbox.svg) on the track that you’d like to remove Note: When you have selected at least one track, the delete icon and the three dots icon will appear

  9. Select Delete

Go to the Recover deleted playlists page here on your computer Note: You must be logged in on the desktop version of Deezer. Following this link on your mobile device will not work 2. Select the playlists that you want to recover
